<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Deep</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

table {
	border-collapse: collapse;
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.collection-title {
	display: inline-block;
	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {
	margin-top: 1em;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	empty-cells: show;
}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table th {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {
	background: rgb(247, 246, 243);
	color: black;
}
.simple-table-header {
	font-weight: 500;
}

time {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

.icon {
	display: inline-flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: center;
	max-width: 1.2em;
	max-height: 1.2em;
	text-decoration: none;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
}

img.icon {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.callout img.notion-static-icon {
	width: 1em;
	height: 1em;
}

.callout p {
	margin: 0;
}

.callout h1,
.callout h2,
.callout h3 {
	margin: 0 0 0.6rem;
}

.user-icon {
	width: 1.5em;
	height: 1.5em;
	border-radius: 100%;
	margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;
	margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;
	margin-left: 0.07em;
}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;

	background-size: 16px;

	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");

}
	
</style></head><body><article id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a310-d372a56476a8" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Deep</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-93b4-d5479d7c2091" class="">12) Expanded catalog (F25–F60): missing loops + equations + tests (exhaustive direction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9f38-f17ca1d0388d" class="">F25 — Observation operator stack (what is “seen”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8578-edb17357aadf" class="">Reality → sensor → encoder → feature map → record.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-8d9e-f7937aef4b23" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t = \mathcal{O}(W_t; \sigma_t)=\phi(\psi(\chi(W_t;\sigma_t)))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b5b0-dc744ad8fc30" class=""><strong>Gate:</strong> any claim must reference which  produced it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-871f-f27aedf24bfb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-ac5f-d7f460275107" class="">F26 — Attention as a conserved budget (time/space allocation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-b055-ec263d6a7046" class="">Let attention budget  be finite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-a501-d8ed74356f48" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_i a_{i,t} \le A_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-9f94-eef0106478ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_{i,t+1} \propto a_{i,t}\exp(\eta \cdot \Delta \text{Value}_{i,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-a73e-ec190b1d6a14"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-902e-c6d07d76ba9c" class="">F27 — Surprise / prediction error loop (micro→macro bridge)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a551-f114e44e80d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta_t = -\log p(O_t\mid M_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b450-fa727959de2c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=M_t+\alpha \nabla \log p(O_t\mid M_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a17b-f4d32e4e8acb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-b166-edb4b0b29f83" class="">F28 — Compression–generalization dual (records that matter)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-bf35-f7400cf14d8d" class="">Let  be compressed description length,  prediction loss.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-bedd-f21a286ab7b6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{M} \; L_{pred}(M) + \lambda L_{comp}(M)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-a021-cb6c2f0a2c90"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-abfa-e247491d609e" class="">F29 — Law of Law operator (meta-law, enforcement layer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-a546-c736c08cf8fe" class="">Define law operator  that constrains allowable transformations:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-b04a-e1012ff8c1f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} \in \mathcal{K}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-99d5-e247b09deed1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
v_t = Dist(x_{t+1}, \mathcal{K}(x_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-9e1a-c0da8f37dba9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-802f-c45bf3019f06" class="">F30 — Rule-of-2 / Rule-of-4 entanglement mapping (mechanical form)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9ac6-c1ff4226ea7b" class="">Rule-of-2: any claim must have a dual decomposition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-810a-e035abda81ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Claim = (Mechanism,\ Boundary)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-9877-c7ed7a0407df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q = \{(Internal,External)\}\times \{(Static,Dynamic)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-bb80-f12361fa0436" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists ! q \in Q: Claim \mapsto q</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-975b-f19e6bb0dba8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-9e0e-e95b6adb667e" class="">F31 — Ownership invariant (information has owners)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a359-dc1ad14b448b" class="">Define provenance graph  with edges “derived-from.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8df0-e85bbcf87115" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
own(y)=\bigcup_{x\in Anc(y)} own(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9c33-cac10defc02d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Use(y) \Rightarrow \Pi(own(y))=\text{ALLOW}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8ca1-ebf2970ff955"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-87b4-cf8313dcd90b" class="">F32 — Memory as a tri-store (episodic / semantic / procedural)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-a139-cac8650a6dad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_t = (M_t^{epi}, M_t^{sem}, M_t^{proc})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-9b9b-e7d28c4cefab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M^{sem}_{t+1}=M^{sem}_t+\beta \cdot Compress(M^{epi}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-ad48-c96488561b0f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b8fb-de1f34cb628c" class="">F33 — Forgetting is not decay; it’s control (active deletion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a14f-e912eaa1bf96" class="">Forgetting policy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-915d-d32ae308f3b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=M_t - \varphi(M_t,\delta_t) + \text{(new writes)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-953f-ccedd13aab61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{erase}\ge kT\ln2\cdot \dot B_{erase}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-90dc-ed5b0c563a52"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-91f7-e26a27abe8f5" class="">F34 — Subconscious as latent state with partial observability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8c93-c3b42e205ea7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_{t+1}=A h_t + B u_t + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-80d6-e074827941f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = \pi(h_t) + \epsilon_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b2f5-d644d233017e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a29e-c7cf2b690c06" class="">F35 — Conscious access as broadcast threshold (discrete transition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-82c3-cbf7b6cff8c5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_t = \mathbf{1}[I(h_t;O_t)\ge \theta_b]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-af7b-d06d611a3a71"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-aa98-f20eae3472f0" class="">F36 — Stability of identity (self-model invariants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-bed0-cfca2d1d1299" class="">Identity vector  must be consistent:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8e48-e21090c169f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|id_{t+1}-id_t\| \le \epsilon_{id}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-9718-d5042d00d656"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b42b-cd5c877585dc" class="">F37 — Emotion as a control signal with costs</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a6ef-c9d687e72c54" class="">Emotion state  affects policy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-8317-da784f2820db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t = \pi(M_t, x_t, e_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a677-f74f0dd23b08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1}=E_t-\kappa_e \|e_t\|^2 + \text{intake}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ae2b-dadba4c4d102"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-b540-c77efc5a2609" class="">F38 — Social synchrony (multi-agent coupling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-93f8-c9b9ba416a52" class="">Agents  with coupling :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-be42-e5b2f1aaeba4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(i)}_{t+1}=F(x^{(i)}_t)+\sum_j K_{ij}(x^{(j)}_t-x^{(i)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b97c-d085faf069cc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-810a-c9ab3ad1b7d8" class="">F39 — Cultural memory as distributed redundancy (civilizational record)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-920d-d99161fdb7f1" class="">Let cultural redundancy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8886-ed8d58cbf1f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R^{cult}_t=\sum_{artifacts} w_a \cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{replicated}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-98c5-c2d0a1ca8539" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R^{cult}_{t+1}=R^{cult}_t+\beta G_t-\kappa \Xi_t R^{cult}_t-\lambda \text{(suppression)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-93df-e24c50168da9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-8c16-f9d123490979" class="">F40 — Technology as exo-cognition (external recursion layers)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-bcf2-cf78bf7a4024" class="">Define external model stack  (tools, writing, machines).<br/>Effective depth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-9ffa-cc9af388feee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D^{eff}=D^{bio}+D^{ext}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a10e-e0512357bea4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D^{ext}\le f(C_t, T_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-bac6-cdcbeeb7e8a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-8bda-d47f2911c552" class="">F41 — Trust as a gate on social information ingestion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-a066-f6f1a413119d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{use}_t = T_t \cdot I^{raw}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-903b-d2b83b2736d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{t+1}=T_t+\alpha(\text{predictive hit})-\beta(\text{violation})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a889-d8782b3a9054"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b851-f2abde0b0d2d" class="">F42 — Noise taxonomy (physical / informational / adversarial)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-b14c-d60d14bd2c4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t = \Xi_t^{phys} + \Xi_t^{info} + \Xi_t^{adv}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-9c00-ef3a6c0dd3cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost(\Xi^{adv}) \gg Cost(\Xi^{phys})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-be0e-ecc2933c2bf6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b3ab-c2e02f7681e8" class="">F43 — Red-team loop (self-attack to seal gaps)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-a8b4-eebbc2d38040" class="">Let hypothesis set , red-team generates counterexamples .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b317-c893ec4a990a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk_{t+1}=Risk_t - \alpha \cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{patch applied}] + \beta \cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{new exploit}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-bf1c-ebe7be5e3de9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-916f-c5e2a28beaf0" class="">F44 — “Intangible” channel formalization (no free claims)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b969-fea673d6bd29" class="">All intangible signals must define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9711-df3985eef84a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-933c-e493b3151bf1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bandwidth proxy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-a8c8-d4db39fce038" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-8cfb-f2b2463d272e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">identifiability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-b42b-f76b689d6007" class="">If missing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-958b-cf1bc51162ea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Claim} \rightarrow \text{LIMIT}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-8393-d47eb96ba330"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-94fa-c242e0fe9938" class="">F45 — Earth coupling (planetary constraint as boundary condition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-aebd-cdcd129f5484" class="">Planet state  (gravity, magnetosphere, climate gradients).<br/>Human system receives:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-ac99-fc6b691dd38c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t = g(P_t, \text{location}, \text{time})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-9d8c-eeb23b5e2943" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t \ge G_{min} \ \land\ \Xi_t^{env}\le \Xi_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-96a5-f1582fb20392"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-85b1-d981d77602f4" class="">F46 — Cosmological boundary as constraint selector (Past Hypothesis generalized)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-bae7-dfb40e28f83e" class="">Instead of one macroregion , allow a family :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9c5b-d814501e14e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t_0)\in \Gamma_\lambda</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-ab97-c1ebc3cac47c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mu(\cdot\mid \Gamma_\lambda)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-ac66-c7a6f7d98952"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-906e-e30088002250" class="">F47 — Birth/death as interface events (record continuity test)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-96d2-e52233438dde" class="">Define organism boundary event  (birth),  (death).<br/>Record continuity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9cfb-f0192c909451" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R^{bio}_{B\to D} \gg 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-b575-cfbe673065fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists R^{ext}: I(\text{person};R^{ext})\ge \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-a8ba-f06823573fc2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-b6d7-cb7024366d96" class="">F48 — Time symmetry vs time orientation (operator distinction)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-a887-d72361dcf6c6" class="">Dynamics may be reversible:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8608-c2cb57a3563d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}^{-1} \text{ exists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9358-cb2823151dbb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Write \neq Erase^{-1}\ \text{under constraints and noise}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b689-c7278a29e0f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-8f82-d60e43f0b747" class="">F49 — Causality audit (no hidden acausal steps)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-af30-dd65af30ebbf" class="">For any inferred causal link :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8066-d186057d55ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(A;B\mid \text{Past})&gt;0 \ \land\ \neg I(B;A\mid \text{Past}) \text{(directional test)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9423-daf2eae1b677"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-9a03-e9180a02c620" class="">F50 — Dimensional/units gate (physics-grade hygiene)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9f63-c29d7f6df46a" class="">Each equation must pass:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8a75-f907fe665c88" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[\text{LHS}] = [\text{RHS}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-ba8d-e9f53f91d784"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-aa86-ef96a8c8b859" class="">F51 — Discretization invariant (simulation cannot invent dynamics)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-bcde-fef83aa580e1" class="">If continuous model , discretization must satisfy stability bounds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-81dd-f5bfbaeb5192" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|x_{t+1}-x_t-\Delta t f(x_t)\|\le \epsilon_{disc}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a3a8-e2176aff9e4a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9419-f802e7adce72" class="">F52 — Multi-resolution hierarchy (micro↔macro closure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-bbe7-df4a97283d08" class="">Coarse-graining map  at scale :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-940f-cd4f83adfc61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(k)}=C_k(x^{(\mu)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-b90e-c0f67116b356" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_k(F_\mu(x)) \approx F_k(C_k(x))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-84cd-c0b192854ab1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-a1ae-f74466c64a13" class="">F53 — Species loop inheritance (learning transfer across generations)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-88ae-c8c96dc701eb" class="">Let trait vector  update with selection + learning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b8e3-fd76e27f4b84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta_{t+1}=\theta_t+\alpha \nabla Fit(\theta_t)+\beta Learn_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-89cb-c5fc12d641e2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-8eeb-d7294d835d2c" class="">F54 — Visual/sound prediction stress test (your request)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-a4b3-f890ed08ac4c" class="">Define multimodal observation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a45a-e6b904abaee6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(O_t^{vis}, O_t^{aud})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-83cf-d570307c922f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat O_{t+1} = f_\theta(O_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-ad05-c7d39fdb2dda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S} = -\sum_t \log p_\theta(O_{t+1}\mid O_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-8204-f8a23747e2dd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-bddc-c3e725546a86" class="">F55 — Hallucination audit (structural gap detector)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-993e-f3da8aac8d02" class="">For any generated statement , enforce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-be44-d6ad17bcd8e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

LoadBearing(y)\Rightarrow SupportType(y)\neq PRIM \text{ unless declared}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-b0c3-e0a53dd3b44a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8b39-cf13e2fb0ead" class="">F56 — Contradiction engine (immune system)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-9b64-eadaade3c626" class="">Maintain contradiction set :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-b4e5-dff0009833ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}_{t+1}=\mathcal{C}_t \cup \{(y_i,y_j): y_i \perp y_j\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-a42b-ede75b74b769"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-9d03-d95a653bec38" class="">F57 — Deterministic ID + provenance for every artifact</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-93a8-f58f6f2ff908" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
id(a)=\mathrm{sha256}(content(a)\Vert schema\Vert parents)[:12]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a552-fb58194ab3de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a2dd-c389da1042c7" class="">F58 — Market/forex engine (bounded compliance) as a loop instance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-b044-e886ed09e0ec" class="">Define price , signal , policy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8878-e23072361d61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

Constraint–Gradient dual (Meta-law operator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-a5d9-d74c1ff5567c" class="">You asked: “correct move is invariants of constraints?” Yes, formalize <strong>dual</strong> invariants:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9ca5-ec0c17df63d8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Constraint density (independent constraints / volume)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a5d6-d99d8c9da7cd"
