id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-8421-c29e775cffb2" class="">Coupled dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-886c-fd0c8bb474eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}=q_t-\alpha_q \Phi_t + \eta_q</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-8110-c6f2174eb11d" class="">G_{t+1}=G_t+\alpha_G(\nabla E_t)-\beta_G,Diss_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-b531-de343a522bd7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi_t=\text{unwinding flux (structure formation + radiative degrees)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b842-d66e6d197a2d" class="">Invariant form (meta-law):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-867d-c9112881689e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta S_{cg}\ge 0 \quad \Longleftrightarrow \quad -\Delta q \ge 0 \ \text{after conditioning on Past Hypothesis}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-a508-fea2467c30b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9ca0-cc31af2553ed" class="">F18 — Multi-scale write-capacity (not one , but a stack)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-baac-f6ed761bdf8d" class="">Instead of one write budget, use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-9ca5-d9ea5b6a8e41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t=\begin{bmatrix}
U_t^{\mu} \\ U_t^{bio} \\ U_t^{soc} \\ U_t^{cos}
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-b1f8-d588db23402b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}^{(k)}=U_t^{(k)}-\gamma_k \Delta R_t^{(k)}+\rho_k \text{(refresh)}-\delta_k \text{(overwrite)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-b0a8-ce1157ee428c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t^{(k)}&gt;0 \ \land\ p_t^{(k)}&lt;p_{th}^{(k)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8e77-d54fe195b42e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-a367-faf726a93056" class="">F19 — Electromagnetic / “channel capacity” layer (WiFi, bio-EM, etc.)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-8cb7-efdf24969d2b" class="">If you instrument an EM channel, define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-8cb3-e3c3872939f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SNR:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-8245-e477180661ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bandwidth:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-96ba-cc8607f45d18" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">channel capacity (Shannon):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b7f1-d35987f8281e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t=B\log_2(1+\mathrm{SNR}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-9fb4-d616a29f4ad3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{acquired,t} \le \int_0^t C_\tau\,d\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-ad37-e8aeaab82997" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal I(\theta) \le \kappa \cdot I_{acquired,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8f54-c098edb0ccbd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CanonicalObsGate (F3)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a706-d159043785a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">IdentifiabilityGate (F5)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-be9a-d1f4172b763e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BudgetGate (compute/storage)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-bf00-c7b89be15274" class="">Anything else must be typed LIMIT/PRIMITIVE.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bc81-f3c57e5b4ed8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-9cf5-f7d99609768f" class="">F20 — Self / non-self boundary (immune analogy, formal)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a355-c223df6ff216" class="">Define internal model  and observation .<br/>Define novelty distance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b9be-cb70415ee8c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d_t = Dist(\phi(O_t), \hat \phi_t) \quad\text{where}\quad \hat \phi_t=\mathbb{E}[\phi(O)\mid M_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9d3d-e58ad7f746aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Self = \{O: d_t\le \tau_{self}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b231-d4286139c2e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d_t&gt;\tau_{self} \Rightarrow \text{ImmuneResponse}_t=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a543-ccfb72472213" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=M_t+\alpha \cdot \mathbf{1}[d_t\le \tau_{learn}] \cdot \nabla \log p(O_t\mid M_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-a823-fd51268a3909"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-ae04-c5ad0f33d291" class="">F21 — Consciousness/awareness reconstruction (structurally bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b64a-fc2e6d2b5107" class="">To keep “no gaps,” treat consciousness as a <strong>model class</strong> with explicit observables and refuse claims that exceed observability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a996-dd40ebd5ee76" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-9fbe-e55ec4ba1a0f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">workspace/broadcast variable (latent)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-8331-dfb88a83a888" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">report variable (observed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-adfd-d9d251465fb9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">integration proxy (observed/estimated; may be LIMIT if not measurable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a1e0-c1d8e3884b6a" class="">Minimal state model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-9184-e43b169f4c1a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{t+1}=f(W_t, O_t, N_t, B_t) + \epsilon_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9907-fa8074933aee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(r_t\mid W_t)=\mathrm{Softmax}(g(W_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-b9a2-f56baf8118a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Claim “aware”} \Rightarrow \exists \theta:\ \mathcal I(\theta)\ge I_{min}\ \land\ \text{predictive lift } \Delta \mathcal L \ge \ell_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-b1cb-ca317101f34e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-83da-e590f7c8f9c5" class="">F22 — Cross-species loop mapping (one operator, many bodies)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-95b3-e5e048ea68fa" class="">Species index . Shared invariants live in a common latent manifold .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a1e3-dd06deff39cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{t+1}=A Z_t + B u_t + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-b65f-ea3423e9c423" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t^{(s)} = h_s(Z_t)+\epsilon_t^{(s)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-ab20-eb01ea834710" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Inv(j)\ \text{holds} \iff \mathrm{Var}_s\left(\hat\theta_j^{(s)}\right)\le \tau_{inv}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-9b7a-cc415a82c031"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bf3a-cad92cb22bd2" class="">F23 — Civilizational control simulation engine (multi-loop, dynamic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-b832-cda59ffdca2a" class="">Let civilization state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-808f-e8db0a9f4abb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t=(E_t,\ I_t,\ R_t,\ L_t,\ T_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-af7d-cdd23b9a6412" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: energy throughput</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b323-c68d98842f30" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: information throughput</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-82e4-c910c6a390c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-812c-f7daf7a861b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: law/governance constraint density</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-a6a5-ef45e0fa9c92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: trust/social capital</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8f14-d26c6a8c1395" class="">Coupled update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-81e7-cccc2c99a416" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1}=E_t+\alpha_E\,Prod_t-\beta_E\,Waste_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-b772-c1e904864d05" class="">I_{t+1}=I_t+\alpha_I,C_t^{chan}-\beta_I,Noise_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-ad29-e790fcb4205a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G_t-\kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9624-ef748f00f8d7" class="">L_{t+1}=L_t+\alpha_L,Codify_t-\beta_L,Erode_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-9234-fe8540be8354" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{t+1}=T_t+\alpha_T\,Align_t-\beta_T\,Betray_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-a6e5-f3341375496d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9a09-fafabe5c5223" class="">F24 — Horizon/thermo “loophole scan” (formal, bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a669-e5a79abc0ef3" class="">You asked if there’s any loophole in horizon-information chain. AMOS treats each possible loophole as a parameterized hypothesis  with a gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8bb2-f869c3a6d416" class=""><strong>Loophole classes</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-9c17-d085156ae7d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Quantum memory</strong> (error correction / topological)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-ab04-f035c9d267d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_{eff}=p\cdot e^{-\lambda d}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-89ca-f94ebc4af5bb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Non-equilibrium reservoirs</strong><br/>Increase or reduce locally:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-82bd-d6c21c147a58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{min}\propto kT\ln 2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-aef7-fb9e6c3851df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Access beyond horizon</strong><br/>If claimed, must define an observation operator and pass IdentifiabilityGate; otherwise LIMIT.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-8394-e378d50c40b4" class="">AMOS output is not “no loophole,” but:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8f05-e2d2e655eef8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">which reduces which gate,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-96ce-e21109b30a17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and what new observables are required.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-a870-d8ffabbdf4ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-929f-f21b124ccb68" class="">9) “All equated parts” — the unified gate chain (single line)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-aad1-ce638d7e2a29" class="">A system can increase recursion depth and maintain stable records only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-992d-efb0820ccca6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\underbrace{U_t&gt;0}_{\text{write capacity}}
\land
\underbrace{\beta G_t&gt;\kappa \Xi_t R_t}_{\text{arrow gate}}
\land
\underbrace{p_t&lt;p_{th}(d_t)}_{\text{code gate}}
\land
\underbrace{P_{avail}\ge kT\ln2\cdot\dot B(D)}_{\text{compute gate}}
\land
\underbrace{I_{records}(R)+I_{models}(D)\le I_{max}}_{\text{memory gate}}
\land
\underbrace{\alpha_d\psi(\tau_d)&lt;1}_{\text{control gate}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9ffa-ca418db5a1b7" class="">This is the “no hidden gap” closure: if any conjunct fails, the system is BOUNDED or INVALID (with reasons).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9d16-c684a9a5c7f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-885b-e15eff902738" class="">10) What you likely still haven’t formalized (high-value missing list)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-b899-cb9a876ea2e3" class="">These are the usual “gaps” that cause hidden invalidity; each is now an explicit module:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9d05-c7efd03039fc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Observer selection operator</strong> : what subset of reality is even sampled?</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-ba5f-e4cbd84da537" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=\mathcal H(\mathcal S(\text{world}_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-908f-dba8f272879f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dimensional consistency</strong> across equations (units gate).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-b48f-cb908a90e6c8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Non-stationarity</strong>: parameters drift unless stabilized.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-965c-e38a6562a1b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta_{t+1}=\theta_t+\zeta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-aa1e-c6799a85605a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Regime switching</strong>: single dynamics isn’t enough → stage-conditioned tensor .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b83b-c4c341515adc" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Intangible claims</strong> without operators → must be PRIMITIVE/LIMIT unless instrumented.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-8d7f-ea37a725a451"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-ba2a-ef4d44a93f66" class="">11) Next deliverable (pick one token)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9746-c5be4e696fac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>FEATURE_CATALOG</strong> → I output a complete <code>feature_catalog.json</code> (all F0–F24, variables, equations, gates, tests).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-89bf-ee5909ac94a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EQUATION_REGISTRY</strong> → I output <code>equation_registry.json</code> + <code>symbol_table.json</code> (every symbol defined once).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-add4-ed8bc3421252" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KERNELSPEC_FILES</strong> → I output the file-level implementation plan (module paths under your 00–17 tree, functions, tests, reports).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-a8f1-c3419914bd5c" class="">0) Scope note (so you don’t get hidden gaps)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-a2f1-e5a09c6a7a3a" class="">You asked for <strong>feature details + equations</strong>. Below is a <strong>closed, implementable</strong> spec: each feature has (a) variables, (b) equations, (c) required observables, (d) gates/failure modes. Anything not instrumentable is explicitly tagged <strong>PRIMITIVE</strong> or <strong>LIMIT</strong> (non-load-bearing) so the system stays structurally valid.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-827b-cdd99436164a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-b63a-c9e85bbfbd4d" class="">1) Grand Unified Feature Stack (what AMOS builds)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9dda-ee7a954595a5" class="">1.1 Kernel features (always-on)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b564-f84e698c951b" class="">F0 — Deterministic identity + provenance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-9cc4-faf8d88a785b" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> every artifact/claim/model has an irreversible lineage and owner.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a7ca-dade0ea39ef1" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-8b19-c55cf4e47d4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: provenance chain hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-a901-e6fa65429edd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: artifact hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9e59-f8fa6464961b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: owner id</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bff0-d5b9a1b48c59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: rights tuple (read/derive/publish)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-a977-d61521de931f" class=""><strong>Equations</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a907-e46ff6ccbf16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Chain:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8bc2-df03fbeb256b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_{t+1}=\mathrm{sha256}(h_t \Vert a_t \Vert o \Vert Rgt(a_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-8d2b-f6fe975bf7c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega(a \rightarrow b)=\mathbf{1}[\text{derive}\in Rgt(a)]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{owner match or grant}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9009-de9c1705200a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b \text{ admissible} \iff \Omega=1 \land \text{parents}(b)\neq \emptyset</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-b268-c2ccc77c670d" class=""><strong>Gates</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-8589-fd20f9b19718" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">OwnershipGate:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-84f8-e8495092dc35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ProvenanceGate: hash chain intact (no missing parent)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-9e02-ead6eb918b8f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a708-d6dbcc19b1ad" class="">F1 — Single-support claim typing (UCIA-compatible)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-9913-e60620a34209" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> every claim is typed into exactly one support class; no analogical load-bearing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9109-ee4c7967b303" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a89e-dedeb75cb51c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: claim i</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-8b79-fc3c24cca94c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8d49-f9a824768e05" class=""><strong>Constraints</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9582-e884a6ce6fbf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{k}\mathbf{1}[s_i=k]=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b9b3-cd55f97647ae" class=""><strong>Support closure</strong><br/>Let  be dependencies.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-84ad-e47c87f6eb57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c_i \text{ closed} \iff \forall c_j\in Dep(c_i): (c_j \text{ closed}) \lor (s_j\in \{\text{PRIM},\text{LIMIT}\})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-bf56-cefba4094e3d" class=""><strong>Gates</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-9a3b-e040df7cc20e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SupportSingletonGate: exactly one support type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8f5e-e4ade622f1c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ClosureGate: dependencies closed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-8aa5-f251cdcf2a89"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-9c1a-f662b634bfae" class="">F2 — Contradiction engine (deterministic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-80d8-f98077bbbe8c" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> detect contradictions across claims, equations, or observed data.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-acc6-c56481527a78" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-aed8-c23b8b5b3b99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: directed graph of claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-ae30-e2220eb9482b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b3ff-e0d3ad73d94e" class=""><strong>Contradiction operators</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-b07a-e286bf50e653" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Logical:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-9a08-c231035465e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Numeric: interval inconsistency</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-8698-f94af912190e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[c_i^{min},c_i^{max}] \cap [c_j^{min},c_j^{max}] = \emptyset \Rightarrow contr(i,j)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a380-fc754f5809c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|f_{\Theta}(x)-y\|&gt;\epsilon \Rightarrow contr(model,obs)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-8a3d-c28a934434cb" class=""><strong>Gates</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9210-ea3bb9b6c8e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ContradictionClosureGate: every contradiction is either resolved, bounded, or escalated as Issue(BLOCKER).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-ac62-f221b204aee9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-86f8-ffa62f927daf" class="">1.2 Sensing features (text / visual / audio / EM)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8c43-f180178fc128" class="">F3 — Multi-modal observation canonicalization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a8ea-f07bd8e0348c" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> unify all inputs into one observation algebra.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-bba0-fb5080fea970" class=""><strong>Observation object</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-b7a2-f2e27287f86b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(\text{modality } m,\ \text{raw } r,\ \phi_m(r),\ t,\ \sigma,\ h)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-a0c2-edfb7b654780" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: noise estimate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-baef-f82a39edc561" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: content hash</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-9bb8-c4db3575040b" class=""><strong>Feature maps</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-a11e-e5b5f3e2f7cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Text:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8a31-fa526b8b7e9a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{\text{text}}(r)=\{\text{tokens},\text{entities},\text{defs},\text{equations}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-b149-d362b0c5502b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{\text{img}}(r)=\{\text{resolution},\text{histogram},\text{edges},\text{hash}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-8c69-d40682598b2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{\text{aud}}(r)=\{\text{duration},f_0(t),\text{spectral centroid}(t),\text{energy}(t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8c89-cf25c21ea856" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_{\text{em}}(r)=\{\text{RSSI}(t),\text{CSI}(t),\text{phase}(t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-9b0b-dbddb1eaf5bd" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-bbfb-f8cf41ac6f7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CanonicalObsGate: all observations have deterministic hash + schema.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9b22-da4f0c80bafe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-ae52-d5d01d66940b" class="">F4 — Cross-modal time alignment (SYNC)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-843a-eae2cdd6e6d7" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> align streams (audio ↔ video ↔ physiology ↔ logs).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-96ff-d73bf0783567" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8cbd-e833fc0ff989" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">streams</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a10f-d8cc6f0059f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">lag</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-ad46-c78d6d7f6633" class=""><strong>Mutual information lag estimate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-865d-fce2102da8dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^*=\arg\max_{\tau\in[-T,T]} I(y^a_t;\ y^b_{t+\tau})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-b4af-e1c72b06a5b1" class=""><strong>Warp</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-931a-e9bb48a32a64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde y^b_t = y^b_{t+\tau^*}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a5a9-e1be0e5285b1" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8f68-f9e2510ca3f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SyncGate: and stable across windows.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-be26-de3c17109d88"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9933-d047eb4d5eba" class="">F5 — Identifiability (can we infer hidden state?)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8e49-d6c54da09eec" class=""><strong>Purpose:</strong> prevents hallucinated latent variables.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8e8f-c0320caa372a" class=""><strong>Fisher proxy</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-aa15-fe89946fddb3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal I(\theta)=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial \theta}\log p_\theta(O_{0:t})\right)^2\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9820-f6f38a6c81c2" class=""><strong>Identifiability condition</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b01b-dae9a82dd132" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal I(\theta) \ge I_{min} \Rightarrow \theta \text{ identifiable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8457-f5300a65a146" class="">If not: latent parameter becomes <strong>LIMIT</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-af7c-c7e47d45a31d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-bffc-f65673df9e1b" class="">1.3 Records + arrow + capacity features</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-be8a-c06b97b0c895" class="">F6 — Record redundancy (operational arrow)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-a6f9-dc4b3d75bed7" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b56b-cb067a703a49" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">system , environment fragments</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-903e-d768c80494f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-acbb-e5143143ac42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(S:E_i)=H(S)+H(E_i)-H(S,E_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b4b3-ed2150dd3025" class="">R_\theta(S:E)=\max{N:\ I(S:E_i)\ge \theta\ \text{for many distinct }E_i}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-bc92-d79ab0c08311" class=""><strong>Arrow criterion</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b8bd-e6bb525073ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}R_\theta(S:E)&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-ad03-e04fffc8d816"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-99fe-e586d8be92c0" class="">F7 — Write-capacity budget (unwritten DOF)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-8e52-fb9bd852c561" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-9e9b-f45ded7a6308" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: remaining write capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-aa43-d42da55d85f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: redundancy increase</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-94b3-e1986eb99ff3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma \Delta R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-aa94-fc724c95e18f" class="">\text{Records possible} \iff U_t&gt;0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-adb3-ca6ee8c714d7" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9b4a-ff4e0465eb5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">WriteCapGate:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bf60-c0410b8550b5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8cad-f911fea6daa9" class="">F8 — Records as error-correcting codes (phase transition)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-a1ba-ee476075904c" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a175-e616f95be9b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">code distance , redundancy , noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-847b-efe003f3d2fa" class="">Threshold condition (abstract):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-bfa2-de7b3ec21641" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t &lt; p_{th}(d_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8946-e3d810723824" class="">Catastrophic decay:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-add1-f282220fea45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t \ge p_{th}(d_t) \Rightarrow R_{t+1}=(1-\lambda)R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-80dd-f0d0346b5824" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-807d-c0e9ed46b5ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CodeGate: noise below threshold.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-89a9-f55b2374a10e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9919-e2a2d8c5fc92" class="">1.4 Recursion depth + meta-control features</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8a90-e4c944def80d" class="">F9 — Recursion depth feasibility (stacked self-models)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-8515-ff55a87808a5" class=""><strong>Levels</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-bf88-fa55fd6dd14c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">models</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-afb3-fbf024cd15a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">error</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-a5f4-db7d60bdaadb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_t=\|m^{(d)}_t-\mathcal T^{(d)}_t\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9c1e-e00de67185f7" class="">\sup_t \varepsilon^{(d)}_t\le \epsilon_d\ \forall d\le D<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-b225-fb1bf2810ad2" class="">Minimal dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-bfec-c3b25da0631b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta_d(t)-r_d(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a432-ce8eafd5f9db" class="">Depth growth condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8675-f5b978dc1316" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb E[r_d]\ge \mathbb E[\eta_d]+(\alpha_d-1)\mathbb E[\varepsilon^{(d)}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-8129-f7047fed2e4d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8541-e89556bf7e1b" class="">F10 — Delay-limited control (the overlooked ceiling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-bebb-caeac7f54b0a" class=""><strong>State</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-9c73-e57433398460" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">delay increases with depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-aa9d-e5facbde450f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{d}=\tau_0+\kappa d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a138-eeb5d12e617b" class="">Discrete stability constraint (generic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-b202-f5cfa39a03fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_d \cdot \psi(\tau_d) &lt; 1
\quad\text{with}\quad \psi(\tau)\uparrow \text{ as }\tau\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8c3d-fb4eb98f8b17" class="">Interpretation: deeper recursion increases delay → shrinks stability region.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-8c4b-cbd1066c381a" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b49b-f40f93e6f7d2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ControlGate: stability under measured</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-978c-f48e9d231233"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-8ed4-cbe77eb7442c" class="">F11 — Thermodynamic compute bound (Landauer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-88ff-e7786fc2d0f2" class="">Garbage bits erased per second :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8854-d2780f42b1fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{min}(D)\ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-96a5-cefb037fead0" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8580-cdd673c4046c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BudgetGate:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-b6f4-d86d8334b7c3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-82c1-e2468439e0d5" class="">F12 — Memory bound (Bekenstein / horizon)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a3c7-fd28234c469c" class="">Bekenstein:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a1fa-cf6914cfe01b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{max}\le \frac{2\pi E R}{\hbar c\ln 2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-bdda-c54737dfa531" class="">de Sitter horizon:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-bab9-e37510da02b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{max}\le \frac{S_{dS}}{k\ln 2}\propto \frac{1}{H^2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-9a03-e332cf2437b4" class=""><strong>Gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-9541-d34475813ef9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MemoryGate:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-a716-ff8394a0d1f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{records}(R_t)+I_{models}(D_t)\le I_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-abf6-f4706f908add"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a651-c720f6da8911" class="">1.5 Constraint-counting (your “meta-law” move)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-bfce-e1979683614a" class="">F13 — Constraint density law (replaces scalar entropy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-9152-d87b29fe506d" class="">Let  be independent constraints per unit volume.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-8412-e5e45e11e687" class="">Accessible microstate volume:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-984b-c38bc9490c03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_t \propto e^{S_{cg}(t)/k}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-8640-d13008f5d4b3" class="">Constraint interpretation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a633-f4186d23bb1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{cg}(t)=S_{free}(t)-\lambda q_t
\Rightarrow
\frac{d}{dt}q_t\le 0 \Rightarrow \frac{d}{dt}S_{cg}(t)\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-aee1-e0c897d2aa02" class="">This makes the arrow a <strong>constraint unwind</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-9c6a-f590f7621bd7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b553-c36842402f0b" class="">F14 — Compressibility arrow (Kolmogorov proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b263-cc9718b19722" class="">Let  be compressed length.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9a7e-e6314cab4983" class="">Record exists when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-bb5c-f6cd3bc74756" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L(C(x_{0:t})) \ll L(x_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-afbc-cbad118c4136" class="">Arrow direction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9151-e86e42563e98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_t \equiv L(x_{0:t})-L(C(x_{0:t}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-af55-ff8313f09e07" class="">\frac{d}{dt}\Delta_t&gt;0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-9229-e1c2a7f55438"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-bb48-d49f5734db58" class="">2) Biology integration (explicit loop equations)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-87e9-c188c640a71a" class="">2.1 Homeostasis budget (dissipative island)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8ff4-c6c71b0a4ff4" class="">Internal disorder proxy :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-8b29-d49a9a41ad22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{int}(t+1)=H_{int}(t)+H_{gen}(t)+H_{in}(t)-H_{out}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-bb4c-ccbc51a15561" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{gen}+H_{in}\le H_{out}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-adb3-eb3ac8f66f18" class="">2.2 Nervous system state machine (minimal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-a803-da31eb839da0" class="">Let  be arousal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-964c-fe4cad6dcbb0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N_{t+1}=N_t+\alpha \cdot Threat_t-\beta \cdot Regulation_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-baa1-ecf6c886492e" class="">Performance coupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-b657-da831982c13f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon_{t+1}=\alpha_\varepsilon \varepsilon_t+\eta(N_t)-r(B_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-bd60-dedfcc646f77" class="">Sleep/repair coupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-acb2-dfb29ce2c923" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1}=B_t+\alpha_B\cdot Repair_t-\beta_B\cdot Load_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-83ed-ef87df40f772"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-a6bb-cd644250bf27" class="">3) “Intangible / spiritual / beyond science” without breaking structural integrity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-80ec-f20b5e3a1444" class="">AMOS can include non-mainstream domains only if they are placed into one of these support types:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-a5ef-ea978d41ac8f" class="">3.1 Primitive invariants (PRIMITIVE)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b94a-c491a2f3070c" class="">Axioms you choose to treat as governing constraints (non-derivable, but explicit).<br/>Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-94a4-d60b9e1c86ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“All information has an owner.” → PRIMITIVE unless you define an observation operator.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8e55-ec1c89c7599c" class="">3.2 Pattern invariants (INFERENTIAL, cross-civilization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-8ab2-de1a621425a1" class="">Define a pattern operator over corpora:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-932e-ce24cf46e4a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi(\mathcal D)=\{\text{motif}_j: \text{freq}_j \ge f_{min},\ \text{persists across epochs}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a16b-fe08e753ce76" class="">Stability score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a657-ec1a2c5df820" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{motif}(j)=\frac{\#(\text{epochs where motif }j\text{ appears})}{\#(\text{epochs})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-b1ac-e421a406e4fd" class="">You can treat high  as inferential support (not empirical causation).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8d7d-ef0c2650d758" class="">3.3 Limit layer (LIMIT)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8c35-f451102322a9" class="">Anything asserted without:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9ac0-ce596f7dfe14" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observation operator ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-ab7e-e8b9aeeaede3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">identifiability ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-8387-ea501456d791" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or falsifier,<br/>must be tagged LIMIT.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b524-e09bfd0f221a" class="">That keeps the system “no gaps” even while exploring beyond science.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b830-ccadcd0967cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-8603-ea570043089b" class="">4) “Full brain / mind / biology replica” feature decomposition (advanced)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b4a1-c92c18851f9d" class="">4.1 What “replica” equals (formal target vector)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-9fce-c202a9537161" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Replica}(t)=\{\hat B_t,\hat N_t,\hat \pi_t,\hat{\mathcal U}_t,\hat K_t,\hat R_t,\hat D_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-a3f8-f7396f8035b6" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a600-e89cd9fcca95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: physiology dynamics model</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-8800-f869d13e9f31" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: nervous system dynamics model</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a510-cbb0154986c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: action/decision policy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b364-c968b7bdac80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: update operator (how beliefs/models change)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-aa5a-f248d5182ffb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: invariant constraint kernel (meta-law + refusal constraints)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-bb7a-fe067a25e634" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record formation dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b1fb-d988c6163b1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-abc3-ed3f5e37cded" class="">4.2 Continuity metric (only way to claim “same you”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b0a8-cf19e2268ab8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_t=w_1\,Sim(K_t,\hat K_t)+w_2\,Sim(\pi_t,\hat\pi_t)+w_3\,Sim(\mathcal U_t,\hat{\mathcal U}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-8cdb-c8b925e2a19a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_t\ge\rho_{min}\ \text{under stress tests}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-9f28-d3a70657d108"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a9ed-f12d022bc09f" class="">5) Stress test suite (visual/sound/prediction) as equations</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-a162-f4f1a437bc5a" class="">5.1 Prediction reliability</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-a6fd-ef3f9588882b" class="">Given forecasts  and outcomes :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-acb4-f5489126f4d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_t = y_t-\hat y_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-80eb-d3406f88dc88" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Rel=\mathbb E[\mathbf{1}(|e_t|\le \epsilon)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b97b-e35e24f3265b" class="">5.2 Intervention / causal gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-b563-cab26ad497ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta u \Rightarrow \Delta y</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-8ecf-fea5763b203b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{sign}(\Delta \hat y)=\mathrm{sign}(\Delta y)\ \land\ |\Delta \hat y-\Delta y|\le \epsilon_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ba97-e9bf762e8555" class="">5.3 Audio-visual sync</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-822e-efa35f4f1430" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau^*=\arg\max_\tau I(y^{aud}_t;\ y^{vis}_{t+\tau})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9176-c2ee8caad276"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b11a-e6435274e353" class="">6) What AMOS should output (concrete feature artifacts)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8910-f6b0904cc80d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>feature_catalog.json</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-b602-e4578081f35c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">for each feature: variables, equations, required observables, gates, tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-9efc-c90cca25650e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>equation_registry.json</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-97cf-ceb42ad1ead1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">all equations, symbol table, dimensional notes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9203-d010c8e0fac3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>gate_registry.json</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-9f59-c436d1d9c683" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">each gate, pass/fail logic, severity on failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-be05-c1ef1052a96e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>stress_tests.json</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-bed3-dd7c262d2ce6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic scoring functions + thresholds schema</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-ab1b-f4b6f9dae757"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-8962-d4098ead819b" class="">7) Next step choice (no questions, just pick one token)</h1></div><div style="display:contents"
