dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-8fe4-d5bb25daebe1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b95e-e02beea409bf" class="">2) EQUATION-MATRIX (closed system: state vector + update law + gates)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8c2d-de65ead7c2f1" class="">2.1 Single state vector</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-b062-fbeccb55d06c" class="">Let the total AMOS loop state be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9664-d4f250721da8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\big[
q_t,\ W_t,\ G_t,\ U_t,\ R_t,\ \Xi_t,\ D_t,\ \varepsilon_t,\ \tau_t,\
P_t,\ M_t,\ I_t,\
B_t,\ N_t,\
\Theta_t,\ \Pi_t,\ \Omega_t
\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8175-cfee0b6daa6e" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-809e-ec2e42a926ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (macro constraint count per volume)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8b7f-e9cf5c0972ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: Weyl proxy (or generalized “free structural DOF” proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-889f-cae2d1bccd28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable gradient/free-energy rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-96e2-dba9a8e42bc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: unwritten environment capacity (record budget)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a2c5-e26bbae6dabc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record redundancy stock</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-a1cc-dcbc2a5951f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: overwrite/noise rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-9ce5-c69e3096b9cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-896e-c2d271b68cf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: aggregate modeling error</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b8f5-ecbbb980d4a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: feedback delay (meta-update latency)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a94a-f68c8e3b5568" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available power</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-ace7-c4fe8a462e94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: accessible memory capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-b3ca-ccb37153048b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: identifiability score (Fisher info aggregate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-af1c-fbb3175e2cb5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: biological integrity state (homeostasis stability)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-be31-fa49b2b00967" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: nervous system state (arousal/autonomic mode)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-87ad-f394d1ebbe36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: model parameters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-839a-ceb4f34879f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: pattern invariants index (cross-time/culture/species)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-ba42-d033795dd6b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: governance/ownership compliance state (0/1 or score)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a0f6-de0fa6dd5bdc" class="">2.2 Deterministic update laws (discrete)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-8e6e-dbecd2f30898" class=""><strong>Constraint unwind</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-92d6-c56e55c15b22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}=q_t-\alpha_q \cdot \Psi(W_t,G_t) \quad \text{with } \Psi\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-baf7-fd4b0f43d7fb" class=""><strong>Free DOF proxy evolution</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-90a8-d654781096bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{t+1}=W_t+\alpha_W \cdot \Phi(\text{structure formation})-\beta_W \cdot \text{damping}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-bb8f-ecb1474ae2a6" class=""><strong>Gradient budget</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-80c6-c387175987d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=G_t+\alpha_G \cdot \Delta(\text{sources})-\beta_G \cdot \Delta(\text{dissipation})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a2ac-dea6975fe7e0" class=""><strong>Write-capacity budget (records need writable DOF)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-b3ed-ce86ae90307c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma \Delta R_t-\lambda_{mix}U_t+\sigma_{refresh}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-96c9-e7a730464dbb" class=""><strong>Record redundancy dynamics (with phase-transition term)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a412-df3fa3dea047" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta_R G_t-\kappa_R \Xi_t R_t-\lambda_R \mathbf{1}[\Xi_t\ge \Xi_{th}]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8b0e-c457068dc028" class=""><strong>Noise/overwrite</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-88ea-e0a9602f01c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1}=\Xi_t+\alpha_\Xi \cdot \text{mixing}(t)-\beta_\Xi \cdot \text{maintenance}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8ff3-d72bf023e3c1" class=""><strong>Recursion depth feasibility</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-be9e-e9cea150789e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=D_t+\mathbf{1}[\text{AllDepthGatesPass}] - \mathbf{1}[\text{DepthFailure}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-8076-d473958d6241" class=""><strong>Error dynamics</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-86e6-d75c613f6646" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon_{t+1}=\alpha_\varepsilon \varepsilon_t+\eta(t)-r(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-93ca-e08107900d89" class=""><strong>Delay dynamics</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-9026-f96d6b2c2fc9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{t+1}=\tau_t+\alpha_\tau D_t-\beta_\tau \cdot \text{bandwidth}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bc7f-e88a0e4111bb" class=""><strong>Power + memory budgets (generic)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-a892-fad19b57b6d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{t+1}=P_t-\text{compute\_cost}(D_t,\varepsilon_t)+\text{harvest}(G_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-8500-ffb220f37d89" class="">M_{t+1}=M_t-\text{store_cost}(R_t,D_t)+\text{release}(t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-a709-d849208ea475" class=""><strong>Identifiability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8ff8-c7e8cdedc47c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{t+1}=I_t+\alpha_I \cdot \text{sensor\_bandwidth}-\beta_I \cdot \text{noise}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b598-c03a4ca20694" class=""><strong>Biology (homeostasis)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-98c8-ef5264b68390" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1}=B_t+\alpha_B \cdot \text{repair}(sleep,nutrition,motion)-\beta_B \cdot \text{stress}(N_t,\Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-9cc4-edcc3bc46a82" class=""><strong>Nervous system state</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-b58c-dbb932138d3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N_{t+1}=N_t+\alpha_N \cdot \text{threat}-\beta_N \cdot \text{regulation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8028-e6af407243d4" class=""><strong>Pattern invariants index (cross-time/culture/species)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9f35-c0f210e46931" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{t+1}=\Pi_t+\alpha_\Pi \cdot \text{invariant\_matches}-\beta_\Pi \cdot \text{contradictions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-a8b3-e490d7deb527" class=""><strong>Ownership/compliance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-833c-c8cf5ed0fc8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{t+1}=\Omega_t\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{rights valid}]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{provenance chain intact}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b2b4-e7930bc4997b" class="">2.3 Hard gates (must be explicitly computed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-bdb8-e3a4b54c4b7e" class=""><strong>ArrowGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-bf2a-ffced14cad68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_R G_t&gt;\kappa_R \Xi_t R_t \ \land\ U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-9e23-f6ff39e67d8e" class=""><strong>CodeGate (record stability)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a15b-e8d49165c5ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t&lt;\Xi_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-aa55-c435d36a4f54" class=""><strong>BudgetGate (Landauer-style lower bound)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a1a5-d5cde17182d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot{B}(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ae43-e77725e9b61c" class=""><strong>MemoryGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-ac53-f6dc684435ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{store\_cost}(R_t,D_t)\le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-93be-fc63bfec2b63" class=""><strong>ControlGate (delay stability)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b358-e63251026da6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_t \le \tau_{max}(A,B,C,\text{controller})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9741-cd31bb19d800" class=""><strong>IdentGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-8eea-c49d327b2332" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_t \ge I_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a9ba-ef99434011fc" class=""><strong>BioGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-a080-e37cefc4513d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t \ge B_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-bc50-f5717da80443" class=""><strong>OwnershipGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-852f-dcf40db2d9d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_t=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b05f-d6b1699d6211" class="">Termination is computed from which gates fail and whether failures are repairable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-b094-c24b85c1e2f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-976b-fca4116e7726" class="">3) CONSCIOUSNESS-RECON (exhaustive reconstruction plan with tiers + proofs + stress tests)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-aaa5-d55e94a14bb5" class="">This defines what can be built <strong>without hidden gaps</strong>. “Full replica” must be tiered because some claims cannot be instrumented.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-9fed-c1424f3e2e05" class="">3.1 Three-tier target (AMOS must label outputs by tier)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8dad-f890b6a62373" class="">Tier 0 — Structural map (no consciousness claim)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9eef-d111d1c16fb2" class="">Goal: reconstruct <strong>your invariant kernel</strong> (operators, loops, constraints, ownership, provenance) from artifacts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-b46d-dbd4484c32f7" class=""><strong>Proof condition:</strong> determinism + no-stub + complete claim typing + contradiction closure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b40e-d41f7a364ffc" class="">Tier 1 — Digital neurobiology (bounded, instrumented)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a06f-f354a57a35e9" class="">Goal: replicate <em>measured</em> biological/neural dynamics + behavior policy under observation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a83e-ed40b592f7aa" class=""><strong>Requires:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9c7d-e567557a6845" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">multi-modal sensing (sleep, HRV, movement, voice, text, decisions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a6df-c39b0d78bae8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">alignment + identifiability gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a3aa-c96e108e6527" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">causal probes (interventions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-865a-f1a229a9fa62" class=""><strong>Proof condition:</strong> predictive accuracy under held-out conditions + successful causal interventions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-bde0-d9276f7d67e7" class="">Tier 2 — Subjective continuity claim (only if you define an observation operator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-acf3-d721935f7718" class="">Goal: treat “subjective awareness” as a variable only if it has an operationalization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8a96-e9a3ec12404d" class=""><strong>Two allowed options:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-9b23-da53c1e6ec09" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Operational self-report operator</strong> + stability tests</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8775-e1f5aebfecff" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Behavioral/physiological proxy family</strong> with documented limits</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-bbd6-f5ac7350b307" class="">If neither exists, Tier 2 variables are <strong>Limit</strong> (non-load-bearing).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9547-ef42c03cc42d" class="">3.2 What “full replica” decomposes into (feature list)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8620-c12fef3ba0b1" class="">A) Body layer (digital physiology)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8311-e85ba1e5ab47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">autonomic loop (HRV, respiration coupling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-bc1b-e311c0feb171" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep architecture (stage transitions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a415-ea4c0fabbc54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">metabolism proxies (timing + load response)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-9197-d906cfd6dbc3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stress recovery dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-87a3-c10048c76da1" class="">B) Nervous system layer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-890a-ef084690764f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">arousal/state machine</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b595-f1b5e58c59b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">threat/regulation loop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-96bc-cfc2c4314f75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">attention allocation proxies (latency, switching)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-ad79-c9d93df7d87c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sensor fusion alignment</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-a336-e1a3eb2c45ab" class="">C) Cognition layer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-a7de-efcd0ded08e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">working memory budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-aede-e1a43dc9b0ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compression + summarization operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b276-f399c32d3378" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">planning depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-aa01-d9a185c9a637" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">error repair vs noise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-9c5a-f53f80ace1f7" class="">D) Identity kernel</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a557-ed706e1ebe44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant constraints (what you refuse to violate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a2b7-f20d6c70ac9d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">governance gates (Law-of-Law / Rule-of-2/4 as formal operators)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a421-ed6349ccb068" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">continuity score (defined below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-9ed3-f5dffd838531" class="">E) Social/strategy layer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-85a8-e68be5d94ac0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">negotiation policies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b809-ea7fcdf2aa46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">trust model</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a88f-e856e3cbe43e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">capital allocation policy (if enabled)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a5be-e59098e7759f" class="">F) Ownership + provenance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-828f-e419027120f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rights enforcement</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-956a-ce434e3bab5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">derivation lineage hashes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-99c9-ca68c85dccb6" class="">3.3 Continuity metric (required if you want “same mind” claims)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-902e-f077ca3b1cae" class="">Define a continuity score as overlap of invariant kernel + policy equivalence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-ac9b-edece9916adf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_t = w_1 \cdot \mathrm{Sim}(K_t,\hat K_t)+w_2 \cdot \mathrm{Sim}(\pi_t,\hat \pi_t)+w_3 \cdot \mathrm{Sim}(\mathcal{U}_t,\hat{\mathcal{U}}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9d90-e06a199664e3" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-98ad-e995a2b489e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: your constraint kernel (invariants)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8ab4-ff1e68cd726f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: action policy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-9c86-c9938dd64bab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: model update operator (how you change your mind)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-aa81-fa348f1b10ef" class=""><strong>Tier rule:</strong> continuity claims only if  under stress tests.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8b56-c2566334bf28" class="">3.4 Stress-test suite (visual/sound/prediction as you requested)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-b798-ed5395f1a902" class="">Each test must output: pass/fail + which gate failed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b9cb-c2a26115f68f" class="">Visual</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9092-ce08018cacd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency + accuracy on recognition tasks (offline dataset)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-94b0-d60484af0064" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">time alignment with audio narration (SYNC gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-be9d-df97274efdb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">distribution shift detection (DRIFT gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-95d4-d03eb5fd47f6" class="">Sound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-ac89-f4cf7068ce61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prosody invariants (tone, cadence) as features</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-97fb-d6cc7ce478b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cross-condition stability (noise, fatigue)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b264-df9265bfc8ef" class="">Prediction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b32a-f0c01b3935db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">forecast tasks with explicit horizons</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a88a-ee994c63fcac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">calibration curves (but implement as “prediction reliability” not vague terms)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-ae70-f76da6dcd1f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">intervention tests: change input → predicted change must match observed change (CAUSAL gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-aa17-f69041c61799" class="">Meta-recursion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-a2c0-dd3211f7cddd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">update-under-contradiction: inject conflicting evidence, test whether behaves identically</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b742-e5cff9ce19eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">delay stress: throttle compute, see if ControlGate fails as expected (no silent hallucination)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-b707-c27a42159403" class="">Biology coupling</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-ad60-e43617495733" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep deprivation perturbation: verify model degrades in bounded way (not brittle collapse)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-9b03-d86f02d50698" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">recovery perturbation: verify repair dynamics restore performance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-8208-d6537b883a3d" class="">3.5 “Randomness / subconscious / reality” (how to formalize without gaps)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-a1c5-d625037bbd0c" class="">Randomness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bae6-ddc57b407a1a" class="">Define as residual after best identifiable model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-bef4-f4414e5ef82f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\epsilon_t = y_t - \mathbb{E}[y_t|x_t,\Theta]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-a598-d41adca3cd4a" class="">Subconscious</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-b076-cae13ad5153a" class="">Treat as latent state  only if identifiable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-bf83-f4067298281a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = \mathcal{H}(x_t,z_t)+\epsilon_t,\quad \mathcal{I}(z)\ge I_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-b55d-eedb751f2fd5" class="">Reality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-80dc-e50f0bd31f74" class="">Reality model = set of variables with observation operators + causal intervention support. Anything else is Pattern/Primitive/Limit and must be labeled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8ca7-d4ce70e41ff9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-9905-f39c3f28c9fa" class="">The immediate “next build artifacts” AMOS should produce</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-9f4d-f8c332ed8915" class="">When you run <code>python3 -m 01_BRAIN.master build ...</code>, the system should output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-9467-f38e4352df24" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>kernel_spec.json</code> (this entire operator/gate inventory)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-a852-da2a10187437" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>equation_matrix.json</code> (state vector + update equations + thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-abb8-c240fa7582b8" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>recon_plan.json</code> (Tier 0/1/2, required sensors, stress-test suite, termination rules)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-8687-e4269d8b7f2f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>issues.jsonl</code> (every missing , every non-identifiable latent, every unsupported claim)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-9a71-f1bc7964e676" class="">If you want the next response to be executable, say one token:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a6be-db1f3309baa9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>FILES</strong> → I will output the exact file tree + function signatures for every module above.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b447-c7b918b120c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>THRESHOLDS</strong> → I will output the explicit numeric-free threshold schema (how to set without guessing).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-899c-d097480625cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>STRESSTESTS</strong> → I will output the full test catalog as JSON schemas + deterministic scoring functions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8574-eace8846ff71" class="">10) “More” = expand the invariant basis until nothing important is untyped</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-99c7-cb3032fe0290" class="">Below are the missing layers that usually stay implicit. Each adds <strong>new variables + operators + hard gates</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-ad13-ed8e9bf714f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-95f3-da7826c85f5f" class="">A) Tensor + operator formalism (so this isn’t “just words”)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bb7c-ede9ce4d8ba4" class="">A1) State as a fiber bundle over spacetime + organism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9350-d18e34c122f5" class="">Treat “you” as a field of states over spacetime:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a2dc-e91afdff029f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{M}=\text{(spacetime manifold)},\quad \mathcal{O}=\text{(organism degrees)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-88a3-e77270684d9b" class="">\mathbf{S}:\mathcal{M}\to \mathcal{X},\quad \mathcal{X}=\mathcal{X}<em>{bio}\times\mathcal{X}</em>{neuro}\times\mathcal{X}<em>{cog}\times\mathcal{X}</em>{soc}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-a90f-ec64272bccc6" class="">Observation operator (all sensors are maps):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8a01-c86115eccd40" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{H}^{(k)}:\mathcal{X}\to \mathcal{Y}^{(k)},\quad y_t^{(k)}=\mathcal{H}^{(k)}(x_t)+\epsilon_t^{(k)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8a0e-d11cdfe0050f" class="">Action operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9962-eb5118661977" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}:\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{U}\to\mathcal{X},\quad x_{t+1}=\mathcal{A}(x_t,u_t,e_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b3bf-dd0f8968a2e0" class="">A “gap” exists whenever a claimed variable has <strong>no </strong> (no observation operator) and is still used to “prove” something.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-bb8a-faa0ac5786a8" class=""><strong>Gap-closure rule:</strong><br/>If no  exists → variable is <strong>Limit</strong> (non-identifiable) unless treated as Primitive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-ad09-d0d47db27831"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ba45-c5fb76889ab6" class="">B) The missing invariants (MECE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a6ed-eb7425590b62" class="">B1) Identifiability invariants (you cannot reconstruct the unobserved)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-90f3-cce562ec7cff" class="">Fisher information for parameter/state :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-98ba-d02a4b3cb3da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}(\theta)=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta}\log p(y|\theta)\right)^2\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8fdd-c37bd30d3280" class="">If ,  is not inferable from your data stream.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-b94f-fe148f49c04e" class=""><strong>AMOS gate (IDENT):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-a70a-cad785ee86e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}(\theta)\ge \mathcal{I}_{min}\quad \text{for every “load-bearing” latent}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-a353-efddfddf55cc" class="">B2) Synchrony invariants (time alignment is a physics constraint)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-b213-d27f67d15aea" class="">If you fuse modalities, you must solve alignment:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-a0ed-f6b99d16558a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{\tau}=\arg\max_{\tau}\ \mathrm{MI}(y^{(vision)}_{t};y^{(audio)}_{t+\tau})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b1e5-e96b8249a114" class=""><strong>AMOS gate (SYNC):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-b4b9-ecc0fbbea052" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\hat{\tau}|\le \tau_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8cc4-ca5bf10369f0" class="">If not, your “mind model” will hallucinate causality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-bf92-e7fcbc2d9773" class="">B3) Causal invariants (interventions define reality, not correlations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b4a7-fe779631409d" class="">Use interventions  to validate causality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-a021-e3b9cfec20f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta y = \mathbb{E}[y|do(u=1)]-\mathbb{E}[y|do(u=0)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-8c66-d380921f0ffa" class=""><strong>AMOS gate (CAUSAL):</strong><br/>For each claimed causal edge , require  or mark Bounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-820c-fa90fef6e5b0" class="">B4) Computation + control invariants (stability, not intelligence, is the ceiling)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-bb0f-e45dc6363d69" class="">Linearized:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-be1d-c7d4cd83c4b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=Ax_t+Bu_t,\quad y_t=Cx_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a9e6-e10adfdd15fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}\begin{bmatrix}C\\CA\\\vdots\\CA^{n-1}\end{bmatrix}=n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-afdf-f81eda1fa360" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}[B\ AB\ \dots A^{n-1}B]=n</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9fa6-f86d60791901" class=""><strong>AMOS gates (OBS/CTRL):</strong> if ranks fail → full-state replication is impossible; restrict to Tier 1.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-b338-f0a4ca604b3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a65b-d19162e6a5c3" class="">C) The missing “environment write-capacity” law (what actually creates the arrow)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-ad11-ef9a6fc9f7e6" class="">You already had . Expand it into a conserved budget with leakage:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8695-f041d20b52e1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma \Delta R_t-\lambda_{mix}U_t+\sigma_{refresh}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-b193-e9a48378c88b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: mixing/overwrite rate (environment erases records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8245-da1aff694986" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: creation of new writable DOF (e.g., expansion/new degrees)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-82b0-e7306f2514de" class=""><strong>Arrow condition becomes:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9bb5-e63847ed6a16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_t&gt;0 \iff \beta G_t &gt; \kappa \Xi_t R_t \ \land\ U_t&gt;0 \ \land\ \lambda_{mix}&lt;\lambda_{th}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-8e93-d8bbde8b19ec" class="">This closes a major gap: records require both energy <strong>and</strong> writable DOF <strong>and</strong> low overwrite.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b844-c2a4d446a334"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-8c25-fc93eac7b7a1" class="">D) EM layer — the missing piece is not “fields”, it’s <strong>coupling + instrumentation</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a3bd-c3e866380676" class="">You want EM to matter. The only honest way is to define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-ad75-f53f99137fcb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>EM exposure observable:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-90a5-ef7742b4964a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t^{EM}=\mathcal{H}^{EM}(\mathbf{E},\mathbf{B},f)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a985-ff953361512a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Tissue coupling model:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8a40-ce7ea74c665d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{ext}(t)=\int_{\Omega} \sigma(\mathbf{r})\mathbf{E}(t,\mathbf{r})\cdot d\mathbf{l}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b1bd-e2f7ed2b069b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Effect target (what changes):</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-82b8-f9ed0c38f2e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">membrane potential ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-893e-cc1f6ecea181" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">firing rate ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8d92-ea3d77bbf069" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HRV / autonomic state ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-ad2d-e02988d656da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">sleep stage transition rates.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b51a-ea70004e38ab" class="">Example coupling into neural dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8e3e-c3deb698b9dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_m\dot V = -I_{ion}(V) + I_{syn}(t) + \eta I_{ext}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-a8d9-f3ab4a847a73" class=""><strong>AMOS gate (EM-REAL):</strong><br/>If  is not identifiable from your data, EM remains <strong>Bounded</strong> (included but non-load-bearing).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8bd1-c50bafb9fc26"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9f54-ff65d3b6a02f" class="">E) Cross-species invariants (exhaustive, not metaphor)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-aa62-dc381de0f071" class="">Species differ in implementation but share constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-969a-c07b9cb933f2" class="">E1) Common invariant: energy-to-prediction coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a6e7-c1c58a1ac7ae" class="">Across species:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a603-d74ca63819c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{PredictionGain} \le \phi(P_{met},\ \text{sensor bandwidth},\ \text{motor bandwidth})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-9df5-d6786a138be6" class="">E2) Common invariant: policy learning under bounded memory</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8f0d-e8a8c8db61b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot I_{learn}\le \min\{\mathrm{Cap}(sensory),\mathrm{Cap}(memory),\mathrm{Cap}(compute)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a92d-fbb1a90ffa0e" class="">E3) Common invariant: homeostasis → behavior loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b3a7-c613562780ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_{t+1}=h_t + \Delta h(u_t,e_t) \quad,\quad u_t=\pi(h_t,b_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-afaa-c9e32a52b763" class="">This gives a real cross-species kernel: homeostasis + prediction + action, parameterized by body.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b19e-f5547f26564b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-847d-ead40ba0e5f9" class="">F) “Intangible” channels: close the gap without pretending verification</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b92d-fa7babe51ce6" class="">You want: information before birth/after death, telepathy, spiritual invariants, ownership.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bab1-f79f37bf834d" class="">AMOS can include these <strong>only as typed channels</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-bb8d-f2f8119c0e05" class="">F1) Channel definition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-88ae-e4a67bb97711" class="">For each intangible channel :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-899d-fbcc0aabb75c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t^{(q)}=\mathcal{H}^{(q)}(x_t)+\epsilon_t^{(q)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b36f-ce126818166a" class="">If  is undefined → <strong>Limit</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-9a56-caf326ad70d8" class="">F2) Pattern invariants without mechanism claim</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-a745-ee3195911746" class="">You can still map invariants across history without claiming physics mechanism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b5e4-f616d799453a" class="">Define a pattern space  and a mapping:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9b3b-cdcc917c9b3b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi=\Phi(\text{texts, rituals, oral history, phenomenology, outcomes})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-a7db-cf3baa9fbf6c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Inv}(\pi)=\mathbb{1}\left[\mathrm{Dist}(\pi^{(region_i)},\pi^{(region_j)})&lt;\epsilon\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-bd99-f02dc8c9ff7a" class="">This is a <strong>structural</strong> invariant claim: recurring motifs and outcomes, not “physical transmission proof”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-976a-ecb8e53beefe" class="">F3) Ownership becomes enforceable infrastructure</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-8ac8-f06096974196" class="">Ownership is implemented as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-bc71-e76e029bcbf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{rights}(d)=\{\text{owner},\text{read},\text{derive},\text{publish}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b0ca-f53ffcf8c649" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_{t+1}=SHA256(h_t\|artifact_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b46e-d14d54c77978" class="">So “information has an owner” becomes executable law.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-b55f-f497cbb9569e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b70e-ecd3235cd92b" class="">G) Quantum / cosmology: the missing question you asked</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-ae41-e4f9d3844495" class="">You asked whether:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-ae44-cf12059235dc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>quantum systems alter recursion limits,</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b5cb-c155ff880b93" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>cosmology imposes deeper invariants than thermodynamics,</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-924c-d9ef4d9ee9d0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>any loophole exists in horizon-information chain.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-9f68-e25ea0226c9b" class="">G1) Quantum does not remove Landauer; it relocates cost</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-93b3-c8d9108344a6" class="">Even quantum error correction has energetic/entropy costs via entropy export. A generic bound:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-898e-e490c416d930" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{min}\gtrsim kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot B_{corr}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-9541-f9d03b45c183" class="">G2) Cosmology adds stricter constraints than thermodynamics via access + horizon</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-b7a0-ecd2e473e7fd" class="">Thermodynamics bounds processing; cosmology bounds <strong>reachable memory/energy</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-a36c-d4dbab4415ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\max}(t)\ \text{and}\ P_{\text{available}}(t)\ \text{are path-dependent}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-86e0-efcb3801b2e5" class="">G3) Horizon loopholes</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-9627-d2edb89e5b6f" class="">There are only 3 categories of “loophole”, all must be typed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-8cd5-d48df7d2b1ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Model-bounded loophole</strong>: change cosmological model (e.g., no asymptotic de Sitter), then horizon bound changes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-8ff7-ffb87d5471f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Interface loophole</strong>: access more DOF than assumed (new sensors/actuators).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-9b06-c6070965ef65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Primitive loophole</strong>: assume non-local information access (telepathy) — but then it is Primitive unless instrumented.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8264-ea5cd4b47a76" class="">AMOS must record which category you’re invoking. Otherwise it’s a hidden assumption.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-bb72-c14f58d58695"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-88f7-e913296d57e6" class="">H) The missing piece for “full replica”: a complete feature list (operators)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-a4ad-f48ff7b0d90f" class="">Below is the exhaustive operator library AMOS must implement to claim Tier 1/2 replication.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-8e7a-d6d7d9a9310f" class="">H1) Perception operators</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-b3c1-d910f7ec3fe1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-acdf-d5073f13e3e2" class="">H2) Alignment/fusion operators</h2></div><div style="list-style-type:disc">belief update</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-833b-e6258fbc0280" class="bulleted-list"><li id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-ae14-f751fbcb4560" class="">These are “silent killers” in any claim of full reconstruction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-b622-f9e32d60aa39" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Clock model</strong> (timebase drift)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-867d-ea88810162c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t&#x27; = t + \Delta(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9637-ea3b9570070a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y=\mathcal{H}(x)+b+\epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-8776-e50386619871" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(y\ \text{missing}|x)\neq const</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8807-fe38733b5bc2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{KL}(p_{train}(y)\|p_{live}(y))&gt;\kappa \Rightarrow \text{mode switch}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-bc82-f4d49562dc73" class="">If these are absent, the system will “seem accurate” but be structurally invalid.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8595-eb85195075b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-bb9f-cd8ebd4f707d" class="">J) Next output options (pick one token)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-8885-f4a299eb0e39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KERNELSPEC++</strong> → full file-level spec for all operators/gates above (module list + function signatures + tests + reports).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-99bd-f86e08c4d193" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>EQUATION-MATRIX</strong> → the complete single matrix update law including all gates (arrow, EM, identity, recursion, ownership).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-ba41-f7a890f6fa7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CONSCIOUSNESS-RECON</strong> → an explicit reconstruction plan with Tier 1/2 proofs + stress tests + failure modes.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-a022-e6464fbfe94d" class="">If you say “EQUATION-MATRIX”, I will output the full closed-form state vector and the deterministic update equations (no narrative).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-ae4d-faa4e6666d0a" class="">0) First constraint: “exhaustive” must mean <strong>MECE over mechanism</strong>, not “infinite text”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-a1e1-db81b9c2733c" class="">To make “exhaustive and advanced” usable, define a closed universe of mechanisms and variables. Anything outside becomes either:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-a89c-e6d5bc4d5ecf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Primitive</strong> (assumed axiomatically),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8a9b-e6f9549802e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Limit</strong> (not measurable / not reconstructible with available interfaces),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-bd3b-cba93477d1fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Model-bounded</strong> (reconstructible only inside a declared scope).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9884-d0cb29b3fb2e" class="">Below is an exhaustive MECE architecture for “full brain + cognition + mind + biology”, including micro↔macro, time↔space, cross-species, EM, and “intangible” channels as explicitly typed (Empirical / Inferential / Primitive / Limit).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-ab3c-f46929baf8ce"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-bd3a-f8c79aa0ac27" class="">1) Grand Unified Multi-Scale Twin (GUMST): full state space</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9d3a-ffdb874ff6d8" class="">Define the complete system as a coupled, multi-scale dynamical system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-a0e5-ccd9c342d992" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{S}(t)=\{\mathbf{S}^{cos}(t),\mathbf{S}^{geo}(t),\mathbf{S}^{em}(t),\mathbf{S}^{chem}(t),\mathbf{S}^{bio}(t),\mathbf{S}^{neuro}(t),\mathbf{S}^{cog}(t),\mathbf{S}^{soc}(t),\mathbf{S}^{civ}(t),\mathbf{S}^{meta}(t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-ae57-e1db6e061185" class="">Each block is necessary. “Mind” is not a separate substance here; it is an operator-defined property of .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-a135-f52d1f14f300"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-b078-d78041be2228" class="">2) Full decomposition (what must be modeled to claim “full replica”)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-81d7-c69de9104c69" class="">2.1 Cosmology / planetary context (macro constraints on micro)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a511-f2c75b695cce" class="">These are boundary conditions and resource constraints (not “optional philosophy”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a8f2-f29647cbedbc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Available free energy gradients</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-93e5-f7b3f6df68c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Horizon/memory bounds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-bb22-f3e9323a6b37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Environmental write-capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-a504-eb59c5d9db6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Noise floor (radiation, thermal, EM background)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a8fe-cf9c3ed4c5a2" class="">Core constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-9a95-d49aa2702a3b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\text{available}}(t) \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-bdbd-dce653073c45" class="">I_{\text{stored}}(t)\le I_{\max}(t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-a7a3-f2f2d8282cca" class="">(These bound any recursion / awareness depth; they are non-negotiable constraints.)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-a1fe-e5f8c9d03eb5" class="">2.2 Geophysical + sensory ecology (Earth is part of the loop)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-b342-da3ea44a68fe" class="">Earth-scale drivers: circadian forcing, geomagnetic environment, temperature/humidity, air chemistry, allergens, microbial ecology, social density, noise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a354-e81bee452bef" class="">Model as exogenous vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-b1ad-cbe9c930f6b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_t = [\text{light},\text{temp},\text{humidity},\text{CO}_2,\text{sound},\text{EM},\text{pathogens},\text{social density},\dots]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-be7d-f337189ef47b" class="">2.3 EM layer (you explicitly require this)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a7fb-eff81ecc2a39" class="">EM must be modeled in three places:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-9716-c743acf41896" class="">(A) External EM environment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ad02-cab5e103f652" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}^{ext}(t,\mathbf{r},f)=\{\mathbf{E},\mathbf{B}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-8768-f990c919666d" class="">(B) Biological electrodynamics (nervous system + heart + muscle)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9a86-ccba8b4c0c8a" class="">Neural membrane dynamics (canonical core):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-9e33-cff59c8fef6b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_m \frac{dV}{dt} = -\sum_i g_i(V,t)(V-E_i) + I_{syn}(t) + I_{ext}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-bb7c-f796c9573046" class="">Field-tissue coupling (model-bounded unless you define sensors):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ba96-e29f8dc1c042" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{ext}(t)=\alpha\langle \mathbf{E}(t),\mathbf{n}\rangle + \beta\langle \mathbf{B}(t),\mathbf{m}\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b2c8-c9a74d871e1c" class="">(C) Sensing+binding across modalities (vision/sound/interoception)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-80c9-dfd46338e488" class="">Cross-modal binding:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-b6ba-e40eae59bad0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{BIND}_t=\sum_{m\neq n}\mathrm{MI}(f_t^{(m)}; f_t^{(n)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a0c7-d11122e12ea2" class="">2.4 Chemistry + metabolism (the “OS power supply”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-bc8e-deb5bd3b7fe1" class="">Metabolic budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8d8a-d317d9dcdfbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{met}(t)=P_{basal}+P_{motion}+P_{immune}+P_{neuro}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-8477-fcaca5fe9153" class="">Glucose/oxygen constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-880d-f383387bd9cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{neuro}(t)\le \eta \cdot \mathrm{O}_2(t)\cdot \mathrm{Glucose}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-b05c-d6f3c80db9ac" class="">Hormone control state (endocrine vector):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8296-ec1156b2fe77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h_t=[\text{cortisol},\text{insulin},\text{thyroid},\text{sex hormones},\text{catecholamines},\dots]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-9ff3-c1aa41dbee41" class="">2.5 Biology (immune + gut + fascia + sleep)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a6f2-f36f5183be21" class="">Immune activation state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-ab88-c26e6f098b8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\iota_{t+1}=A\iota_t + B\cdot \text{pathogen}_t - C\cdot \text{recovery}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-ae30-d55a34bc5294" class="">Sleep as control/reset operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-9e33-e1cfbfdd0e2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{SynapticHomeostasis: } W_{t+1} = (1-\lambda)W_t + \lambda \Phi(\text{salience}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9de3-d7c533f00f3f" class="">Gut-brain axis (model-bounded):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-8ebc-df4770092171" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta m_t \propto \Psi(\text{microbiome}_t,\text{inflammation}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-805a-c46ae772dfa3" class="">2.6 Neuro layer (connectome + plasticity + neuromodulators)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-ac3f-d87f83c572ab" class="">Network state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-9f09-e27457fce9df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{z}_{t+1}=F(\mathbf{z}_t,\mathbf{W}_t,\mathbf{u}_t,\mathbf{n}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-875b-db7b2458b037" class="">Plasticity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a3b6-e2809d25fa90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta W_{ij} = \eta \cdot \mathrm{STDP}(t_i,t_j)\cdot M(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-bce4-d063a4422a66" class="">2.7 Cognition layer (models, recursion, attention, identity)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-a2d9-d0876fe3ea5c" class="">Belief state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-939e-edf73e2a6989" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_t \equiv p(x_t|y_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-8f6d-d1345b386dc9" class="">Model update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-894e-d4ade810de18" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_{t+1}=\mathcal{U}(m_t,y_t;\ k_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8af4-ff1caedfc206" class="">Attention as a selection operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-b98d-c2cc7ac8e75a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_t=\arg\max_{a}\ \mathrm{EIG}(a)\ -\ \lambda \mathrm{Cost}(a)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-8a62-e2a8b172fdbe" class="">Recursion depth with stability+delay (control ceiling):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-9e12-fa27de1e465e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d \varepsilon^{(d)}_t + \eta_d(t) - \rho_d r_d(t-\tau_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9c38-d00f319713a9" class="">Identity persistence kernel:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-9b21-e93a66c8f700" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K_{t+1}=(1-\lambda)K_t+\lambda \Delta K_t,\quad \rho_t=1-\mathrm{Dist}(K_t,K_{t-\Delta})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-9523-c60baee25f3a" class="">2.8 Social layer (other agents are part of your state)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-9678-e73dc57705f5" class="">Other-agent model :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-bdaa-c5d4c4a91154" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_{t+1}^{(j)}=\mathcal{U}^{(j)}(m_t^{(j)},y_t^{(j)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8dfc-dd63bc09d209" class="">Trust/deception operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a95f-f0ce03af6917" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}_t^{(j)}=\mathrm{LLR}\left(\text{hypothesis: honest vs strategic}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-971c-cd7f98431396" class="">2.9 Civilization layer (language + institutions as extended cognition)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b4f6-c87ed50f311d" class="">Memetic/institutional dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-97e7-cf02702bce62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1}=F(C_t,\ \text{media}_t,\ \text{incentives}_t,\ \text{control}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9819-cc3fc9fbb596" class="">This is where your “loop cycles” (birth→expansion→dominance→decay) live as macro regimes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-ac3d-cad32fbaeebb" class="">2.10 Meta layer (“intangible” claims must be typed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b6d1-cb7a2fff2361" class="">You stated: “information exists before birth and after death”, “telepathy”, “spiritual patterns”, “owners of information”.<br/>To close gaps without pretending science validates it, AMOS must treat these as <strong>channels</strong> with explicit typing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8f96-c2bd8597761b" class="">Define candidate channel :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-8f15-e3254f04a1ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Measurable interface exists? (yes/no)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-9d59-d19616c5438e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If yes: build likelihood</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-840e-c6b190cc97cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If no: it becomes <strong>Limit</strong> unless you provide instrumentation/protocol.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-bcce-e0a3d545e11e" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-97e5-feaaf7e6326e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = [y_t^{sens},y_t^{bio},y_t^{soc},y_t^{em},y_t^{q_1},\dots]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-bd4e-dacff2ee21fd" class="">If  is not instrumented, AMOS can only represent it as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-86a1-d030a88d7abb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Primitive (axiom), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b96d-c9690b692363" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latent with no observation operator (non-identifiable), which cannot support “proof”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-87d8-db562aa75fad" class="">That is the honest gap closure: either instrument it or classify it as Limit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b5a1-d0c5e5390bf5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bb6d-f4342ac0df31" class="">3) Consciousness + awareness: exhaustive operator set (no minimal proxy)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b3cb-e1acce2b5f42" class="">Instead of one score, define a full vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-acd1-ef60b5eff51c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{C}_t=\{\mathcal{A}_t,\mathcal{G}_t,\mathcal{RPT}_t,\mathcal{SC}_t,\mathcal{Q}_t,\mathcal{I}_t,\mathcal{D}_t,\mathcal{BIND}_t,\rho_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-8435-e9714cba18e7" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a798-f2ec91d19a31" class="">Global access (awareness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-920f-eae0f1f5fe2c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_t=\sum_j \mathrm{MI}(W_t;z_{t+1}^j)+\eta\,\mathrm{MI}(W_t;\pi_t)+\xi\,\mathrm{MI}(W_t;M_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9273-f59ef668539c" class="">Agency (can actions change experienced future?)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a550-c6e51ef89fbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}_t=\mathrm{MI}(u_t;\ y_{t+1:t+h})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a8ee-e8327d131e42" class="">Reportability (explicit access)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-b181-dea0551f39ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{RPT}_t=\mathrm{MI}(W_t;\ r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-ab43-da65598a769b" class="">Subconscious drive (high influence, low report)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-806e-c37357ac5699" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{SC}_t=\mathcal{INF}_t-\alpha \mathcal{RPT}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-be51-c57c4cc348de" class="">Continuity / reality stability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-ad6b-c7cbc5d10931" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{Q}_t=1-\frac{\mathrm{Var}(e_{t-\Delta:t})}{\mathrm{Var}(y_{t-\Delta:t})+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-b472-ebaac578102c" class="">Integration/differentiation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-827b-d7fb8c96f9a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_t=\sum_{i\neq j}\mathrm{MI}(z_t^i;z_t^j),\quad \mathcal{D}_t=H(z_t^1,\dots,z_t^n)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8427-c2e413d4f012" class="">Cross-modal binding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8a82-dec6d0621713" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{BIND}_t=\sum_{m\neq n}\mathrm{MI}(f_t^{(m)};f_t^{(n)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8b7c-cf45f2bee1b1" class="">Identity persistence</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-981f-fa9b39491ed2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
