capacity)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a7cb-ea020935cbe3" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Scale bridging</strong> (coarse-graining + invariant tests)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b59a-c8c5e5deceee" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Causality</strong> (lineage-based, deterministic)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9336-d0642a401dc3" class="">This operator set closes the key gaps:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-ba61-d101190046c1" class="">5) What was missing (now closed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ae77-f41726fa6f5e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-ba5c-edcc54ed1afc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{PredictGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}[\hat\Pi_t \ge \Pi_{min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-b758-e05ce40889f7" class="">Gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-970b-e8f84d45457b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Pi_t = 1 - \frac{\mathrm{MSE}(\hat y_{t+h},y_{t+h})}{\mathrm{Var}(y_{t+h})+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a564-ebfae19024ca" class="">Define forecast target , predictor . Skill:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-90eb-d79eea05a968" class="">4.3 Global prediction operator (forecast skill)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9182-c5a424eee840" class="">Same as above, using transcript + audio features.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8a7f-fa4327b7f31f" class="">4.2 Audio prediction gain</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-86ad-d1d305c6c6c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If is not stable under resampling, the visual operator is not reliable.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9b59-c925e1e6dd3e" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a094-ee0400c7679b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\Delta Acc}^{img} = Acc(f_1)-Acc(f_0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-836a-fb5f12a90fa0" class="">Train a deterministic baseline predictor  and improved  (same data split, same seed policy).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-af56-c9030a24faf5" class="">4.1 Visual prediction gain</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-8a13-f2057644274c" class="">4) Stress-test operators (visual / sound / prediction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9106-f89538e0b497"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-86cb-f2ab1f2f7891" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{AdversaryGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\forall v\in\mathcal{V}_{det}:\ \mathrm{GatesPass}(v)\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9bdd-d4d35d03c9ec" class="">Given detected threat class :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-a473-ebf8f38ca600" class="">3.6 AdversaryGate (robustness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a671-d088331721ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{MemoryGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\hat R_t + \hat M^{work}_t \le \hat M_t\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-b2e4-f4b26c8cea29" class="">3.5 MemoryGate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b32d-ebaf0f713a42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{BudgetGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\hat P_t \ge \widehat{\dot B}(D_t)\cdot c_{erase}\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-97c6-db8dff19db89" class="">You can’t measure  in software, but you can enforce an accounting inequality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-821f-f9d6c699ec2d" class="">3.4 BudgetGate (Landauer-style lower bound as a required accounting)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8363-ca9146953c89" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{ControlGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\alpha_d &lt; 1 + \gamma_d\phi(\hat\tau_d)\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-aab1-ebd1eb06f2e9" class="">Estimate update delay  from logs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-94a3-da056de11e8d" class="">3.3 ControlGate (delay stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-bd28-d694643c5910" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{CodeGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\hat p(\Xi_t)&lt;p_{th}(r_t)\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-bf3f-d856808576bd" class="">Define noise probability estimate  from residuals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8847-ca33c41d8b8b" class="">3.2 CodeGate (record stability threshold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-82cb-ecd7ac201c70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{ArrowGate}}_t=\mathbf{1}\left[\beta \hat G_t &gt; \kappa \hat\Xi_t \hat R_t \right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-82d9-fb1047c76d6a" class="">3.1 ArrowGate (records grow faster than erosion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-bddb-d45813caf723" class="">3) Gate operators (must be explicit and testable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-9bd8-ffd8ad7eff34"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-bce4-d70ee9a07278" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If an invariant fails under multiple values, it is not invariant; mark <strong>ScaleBreak</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b4cd-e16867395704" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-a150-ec0a0393d79a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathrm{Inv}}(I)=\mathbf{1}\left[|I(\hat x^{(\ell)}_t)-I(\hat x^{(\ell+\Delta)}_t)|\le \tau_I\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-abf1-e3977df010d7" class="">Invariant test:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-bcd3-c24f925b0756" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x^{(\ell+\Delta)}_t = \widehat{\mathcal{R}}_{\Delta}(\hat x^{(\ell)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8039-e91ce23807c7" class="">Define a coarse-graining map on state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-bc4c-ec35fabbfb6e" class="">Purpose: enforce cross-scale invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-80f2-e84140e083a0" class="">2.14 Scale operator (micro↔macro bridge)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b0ad-f543ef328157"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-bb2a-cd33c5e5b279" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If lineage graph cycles appear, mark <strong>CausalCycle</strong> and invalidate affected records.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-967b-c5d15542df3b" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-90d0-f9d679b53c23" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathcal{Causal}}_t = \frac{\#\text{records with valid lineage}}{\#\text{records total}+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-8fd6-d16feff15f84" class="">Aggregate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8df2-c789d268d3b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathcal{Causal}}(A\to E)=\mathbf{1}\big[\mathrm{PathExists}(A\to E)\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-ac8f-c905238a54ed" class="">Causal confidence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-aa7c-cf64e54c2601" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">use deterministic ordering key: content hash lineage + dependency graph + monotone log index (not wall clock)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-bcf6-dde8989543c4" class="">For event  and record candidate :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a6c0-c140066e0f39" class="">Purpose: prevent “records” from being counted when order is ambiguous.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9bf7-c57a429f8505" class="">2.13 Causality (record implies order)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-b046-d0d384e2ebe1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8034-ab3f-c7dd588ca074" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If audits cannot reproduce the same ACL score deterministically, mark <strong>ACLNonDeterminism</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-994b-eecf96e0e007" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-ab28-f222671e748f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathcal{ACL}}_t = 1 - \frac{\#\mathrm{AccessViolations}_t}{\#\mathrm{AccessAttempts}_t+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b795-dee121a0ee01" class="">Ownership constraint score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a7cf-c58dfba1011b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\dot I}_{acc}(t)=\sum_{i} perm(s,i)\cdot \widehat{\dot I}_i(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8b34-c6613b3be69b" class="">Accessible info rate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a2b4-d761aa9c3899" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">permissions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-bc60-e5fb8e372b64" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">owner</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9488-ce4e79dfd0dc" class="">For each info item :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-9289-e335ed195dc0" class="">Purpose: operationalize “information has an owner.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a6fa-caaf2d952544" class="">2.12 Access/ownership</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b2e5-f16a5a882cf4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-ae15-e7d2cefcfdc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If high but system shows frequent collapse events, mark <strong>BoundaryMetricInvalid</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-acee-dd7e1ef161bf" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9026-ff8c688c2870" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\text{Self}}_t = \mathbf{1}[\hat B_t \ge B_{min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8079-a8ac-f912399f8163" class="">Self gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-b581-c93d2a0cc2fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">system: from tests + audits; from failing gates; from uncontrolled dependencies</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-a5ee-d336582d3c7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">biology: from sleep + nutrition proxies; from stress load; from inconsistent routines / instability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b5b9-e3ad7b1ce448" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-8dcb-c29445f869d2" class="">\hat B_t = \mathrm{Clamp}\left(\hat B_{t-1} + \widehat{\dot B}_t,\ 0,\ 1\right)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9b6c-fe657e478c29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\dot B}_t = \hat r_t - \hat \ell_t - \hat d_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9a51-c1c45424c491" class="">Define repair resources , leak , damage :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-87ca-e92b6c7eff32" class="">Purpose: formal self/non-self across species.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-a476-d1a7c24e9efc" class="">2.11 Boundary integrity (self vs non-self maintenance)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-ba24-d087f20a8406"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-923b-ed9af0301ed0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If rises but stability/performance worsens, mark <strong>SynchronyMaladaptive</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-a08a-e34d25822c9d" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8596-dae3120f11a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Psi_t = \mathrm{MeanCoherence}(\text{channels}; f\in \mathcal{F})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-96e0-eab14b434f5e" class="">If you don’t have phases, use cross-spectral coherence proxy across channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-b214-c9b76c191569" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Psi_t=\left|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^N e^{i\hat\theta_j(t)}\right|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-91a0-ee8a820c8948" class="">If you have phase-like signals (EEG/HRV phase, EM phase, rhythm features), estimate phases  then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-8a31-f263ba28b188" class="">Purpose: quantify system-level synchrony without ambiguity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-83c9-e78f2f61324a" class="">2.10 EM synchrony (coupled oscillator coherence proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-82a8-d72893de13e5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-b415-f01db21505f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If capacity predicts improved classification but accuracy unchanged, mark <strong>CapacityNotBinding</strong> (means the bottleneck is elsewhere).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-8271-dc2f7c9632c0" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-968d-d74ec444d287" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM: bandpower peak / background</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-a636-f73114853f60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">vision: contrast-to-noise ratio + motion blur penalty</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-8311-ee3bb7837a1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">audio: signal bandpower / noise floor</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-ae89-e0a93124e9e7" class="">SNR estimators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-b3c3-f7c1cb1b17c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{\mathcal{Cap}}_t = \sum_m \hat C^{(m)}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-b876-f96b1a48f933" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat C^{(m)}_t = B^{(m)}_t\log_2(1+\widehat{\mathrm{SNR}}^{(m)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-8171-cfc616817a43" class="">For each modality :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9455-fa7e977905a3" class="">Purpose: unify sensory limits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-918c-ca8d7f818ae5" class="">2.9 Channel capacity (vision/sound/EM)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-9a77-c78932804d3b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-aae0-e7a67933d28f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If memory claims exceed actual storage or index is non-reproducible, mark <strong>NonDeterministicIndex</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9e24-f7eebedc5ea5" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-9bcd-f63973682ec9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat M^{work}_t = \mathrm{ActiveContextTokens}_t + \mathrm{ActiveFilesBytes}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b602-eddaa422601d" class="">Working set estimate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-82cc-d3c97dd14645" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat M_t = \mathrm{BytesFree}_t + \mathrm{BytesIndexed}_t - \mathrm{BytesCorrupt}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-982b-e129ed3106ad" class="">Purpose: “how much state can persist?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-992a-cbff6fb8bab4" class="">2.8 Memory ceiling (usable storage + working set)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b957-d0422baeaa24"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-b0b2-c9b98b89ecaa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If high but throughput low, mark <strong>BudgetLeak</strong> and require an issue with leak localization.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-9783-f90c9cdaed1d" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-9d2f-e4c6d500ba18" class="">Uncertainty from measurement noise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-abd4-e278bbf441ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat P_t = w_{cpu}\cdot \mathrm{CPUTime}_t + w_{human}\cdot \mathrm{AttentionMinutes}_t + w_{bio}\cdot \hat g_{bio,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-a528-fca5c30765a6" class="">In AMOS terms (offline), treat as budget vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a80f-fc3a7e67d97e" class="">Purpose: “what’s the usable power for compute + repair?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-abee-ddff36a39eb8" class="">2.7 Power budget (available work rate)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a510-dc28c4a7db50"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-8ce3-e8419e307f88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If higher-layer updates improve training loss but degrade out-of-sample stability, mark <strong>MetaOverfit</strong> and cap .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8a9f-c4cccbbcdb4a" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-b33a-f785076ed423" class="">Uncertainty: compute distribution over windows;  from window variance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-afa8-f13c893e7050" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\epsilon_d \leftarrow \epsilon_d \cdot \phi(\tau_d),\quad \phi(\tau)\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-84b7-d801e6fa4408" class="">Include delay stability penalty:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-aefd-c9eed914a977" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat D_t = \max\left\{d:\ \sup_{\tau\in[t-\Delta,t]}\hat\varepsilon^{(d)}_\tau \le \epsilon_d\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-93af-c4ac3319cb68" class="">Depth estimator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-bfb6-e5b8a398c253" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\varepsilon^{(d)}_t = \mathrm{Loss}\left(\hat y^{(d)}_t,\ y_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-822f-ecf2887426c6" class="">Error per layer (must be computable):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-bfcc-e341a85f97da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">etc.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-9a56-c1f85c4d9d2e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy optimizer / auditor</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-be66-e35e258c95c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: model updater</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8c6d-d995e0886541" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: model of world</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-81bf-f7d58f9f7635" class="">Define measurable layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-95cc-df27571f0936" class="">Purpose: “how many nested update layers are stable?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-8351-df9e74186c29" class="">2.6 Recursion depth (stacked self-model layers that stay bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-ac58-c7a7d479b6ad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-8efc-f0a6474c07c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If redundancy rises but retrieval fails (cannot reconstruct from fragments), mark <strong>RedundancyIllusory</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-aadd-d0e030e69a4c" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9cae-cda126de739c" class="">Uncertainty: vary  and compute sensitivity curve; store .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8bbe-f610f75efeed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stable}(E_i,\Delta)=\mathbf{1}\left[\mathrm{Churn}(E_i;\Delta)\le \tau_{churn}\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b869-e8681ddf33d7" class="">Stability test:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9556-d24b158cfb31" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat R_t = \sum_i \mathbf{1}\left[\widehat{I}(S:E_i)\ge \theta\ \wedge\ \mathrm{Stable}(E_i,\Delta)\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-b656-e495b69b1ab0" class="">Redundancy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-805a-c7284aa754dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\widehat{I}(S:E_i) = \alpha\cdot \mathrm{SimEmb}(S,E_i) + (1-\alpha)\cdot \mathrm{SimHash}(S,E_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b21b-f10aa5a3a873" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compute mutual information proxy by shared hash/embedding similarity + linkage:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-93d9-e4eb6c1764a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Partition environment into fragments (filesystems, notebooks, caches, summaries, derived artifacts).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-880d-c7296aadbb56" class="">Operational definition (redundancy across fragments):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-ab49-caa7c0e7a74c" class="">Purpose: “how many independent durable copies exist?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ce-aebb-ccff62c74854" class="">2.5 Records (redundant stable traces)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b4c8-c593bcac63b3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-aaf0-f36bc9e4efc9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If adversarial alerts do not correlate with any observable degradation and are uncalibrated, mark <strong>FalsePositiveAdversary</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-a6fe-f4a0d7fda8f8" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-b93c-fa078fb053f6" class="">Uncertainty: bootstrap over detection thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b0e1-db40266ced43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Xi^{adv}_t = \lambda_1\cdot \#\mathrm{PolicyViolations}_t + \lambda_2\cdot \#\mathrm{InjectionAttempts}_t + \lambda_3\cdot \mathrm{AttackSurfaceScore}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-81af-c00cac54af91" class="">Adversarial noise operator (from logs + red-team signals):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a520-c6a4bb69432f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Xi^{stoch}_t = \mathrm{Var}\left(y_t - \hat y_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-a24e-d76c3cf70ad5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">volatility of sensor residuals:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b8e0-dada7ef6af47" class="">Stochastic noise operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-a4bf-d05dad835d81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\Xi_t = \hat\Xi^{stoch}_t + \hat\Xi^{adv}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-876b-f97a259cb13f" class="">Decompose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-90db-f3fe70812906" class="">Purpose: “what erodes stability?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8bf2-d0b3d85001d8" class="">2.4 Noise / disruption (stochastic + adversarial)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-b2a3-e40d4e56ece7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-a861-c57bc58153c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If stays high but overwrite events spike (detected by churn), mark <strong>WriteCapacityOverstated</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-860c-e438f4fbff69" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b21d-ed724de2d94b" class="">Uncertainty from filesystem/budget measurement error + .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8d37-cc8090be6bb2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record volume (below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8847-f73cee3636bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: external durable capacity allowed (offline disk, paper logs, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b6e2-e79d40ddef7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: free storage in system + working memory budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a84d-f6a8273c2a18" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-81a6-dba502d20276" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat U_t = \hat M^{free}_t + \hat M^{env}_t - \hat R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-ba1a-c71952f7889e" class="">Storage capacity proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-8db3-dae1fcdb175d" class="">Purpose: “how much ‘fresh’ capacity remains before overwrite dominates?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8f5b-c37848222b67" class="">2.3 Write-capacity (unused degrees that can store new records)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a3cf-ce183ba39c2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-ba0a-e2169efc638b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If predicts performance improvements but observed performance is flat/negative across multiple windows, mark <strong>GradientMisestimated</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9f39-e436e44ffc4b" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-8649-e25f72d50264" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\sigma_G(t)=\sqrt{\sum_j (w_j\hat\sigma_{g_j})^2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-8d18-cfd0898c9bfa" class="">Uncertainty:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-ad0c-d091ac482141" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat g_{res,t} = \frac{\mathrm{FreeTime}_t}{\mathrm{Demand}_t+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-b658-edb052d3535d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat g_{info,t} = \mathrm{KL}\big(p_{t}(\text{topics})\ \|\ p_{t-\Delta}(\text{topics})\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9b3c-f1fa9bb90a6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat g_{bio,t} = a_1\cdot \mathrm{HRV}_t - a_2\cdot \mathrm{SleepDebt}_t - a_3\cdot \mathrm{InflammProxy}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a0b2-d52e39e3b810" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bio gradient from physiology (proxy):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-b25d-fc9d906c06de" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-b4c7-c4b4244a3a75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat G_t = \sum_j w_j \cdot \hat g_{j,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-aa7c-c72e6e05171b" class="">Define gradient sources  (metabolic, thermal, informational, institutional, resource).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a3f1-c61de8442a8c" class="">Purpose: “is there a usable difference that can do work?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a416-f8b860e94856" class="">2.2 Gradient availability (free energy / exploitable potential)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8601-e16f13f93897"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-b5a5-dc60ca1d7948" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If adding more data increases constraints but <em>also</em> increases contradictions without resolution, operator must flag <strong>InvalidConstraintSet</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-9e04-d73f81df9e6a" class="">Falsification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-97fa-e604b8159654" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\sigma_q(t)=\mathrm{StdBoot}(\hat q_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-bf58-c24febe26ad9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bootstrap over extracted constraint candidates:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-b342-ec5a75f7bfa1" class="">Uncertainty:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-8c8c-f0422cabfae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat q_t \approx |\mathrm{MaxIndependentSet}(G_K(t))|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bb45-dc83ea32961e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Estimate independence by maximum matching / matroid proxy:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-b663-ee21c31b34f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Build a constraint graph with nodes = constraints, edges = dependency/overlap.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a881-e07b3367142d" class="">Practical estimator (when Jacobian not explicit):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-9780-d392ec95cb65" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat q_t = \mathrm{rank}\left(\mathbf{J}_t^\top \mathbf{W}\mathbf{J}_t\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-83db-d9e3f081ad41" class="">Operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a7c8-d3deb57de977" class="">Define a constraint set  extracted from data (rules, policies, invariants, physical limitations).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8701-fa6f763f9ddc" class="">Purpose: “how constrained is the system right now?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-935e-f5eb3cf86e5e" class="">2.1 Constraint density (count of independent restrictions)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-af85-c98aac8e73a6" class="">2) Core loop operators (the state vector)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-ab73-db0c0df98a43"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-bb05-cc1be73e4dfd" class="">All downstream operators consume only normalized streams.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bdb4-fbb4b8bad1c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: canonical event schema, hash IDs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-acbc-cf3d4f1e5c59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: artifact removal, z-score per individual baseline, window aggregates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a1f0-f6844a83d0b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: resample, compute bandpower features and stationarity stats</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-8b07-e820b0d3c55c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: resize to fixed dims, compute embeddings (offline model) + edge/optical flow</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-933b-cfafe8547ccc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: resample fixed rate, compute log-mel, align transcript segments</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-8504-c95d2a352d67" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: UTF-8 normalize, strip, segment into sentences + tokens</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-abac-e3afd059200c" class="">Normalization (deterministic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-85e6-c884650fad07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: environment context (location coarse bins, weather if offline-cached, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a58c-d45c9617ab54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: social interaction events (messages, trust events, roles)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-831c-eefc9b9da39b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: system logs (files, git, commands, timestamps-for-logging-only)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-ab18-dc6c3b7c0cf0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: physiology (HR, HRV, sleep, respiration, temperature, motion)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-8267-ec191582922d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: EM proxy (wifi RSSI logs, RF spectrum snapshots, magnetometer/EMF sensor logs if present)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-b87f-d0ef37aff3b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: images / video frames</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-bebc-ca13859595b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: audio waveform + transcript</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-9ae6-dfdec8351d74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: text (docs, web dumps, chats)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-81f9-f684c8dcfddc" class="">Define canonical streams (you can add more, but keep these names stable):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-aefb-c032a1eb0b28" class="">1) Inputs (sensor streams) and normalization operators</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-9620-de012a30245b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-b052-e9341a274a7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>falsification_test</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-83d6-fa8fb7ca4fff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>failure_mode</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-8029-cdb8586b85a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>assumptions</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-9543-f27caecbeef0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>support_type</strong> ∈ {Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Model-bounded, Primitive, Limit}</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-b0a2-ebf915f93b47" class="">Each operator must also emit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-8d00-f32043d965a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat\sigma_X(t)=\mathrm{CI}(\hat X_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-9626-d802627f7ba2" class="">and an uncertainty measure (choose one and standardize across AMOS; below uses empirical CI):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9026-fcf9cc8bc7f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat X_t = \mathcal{O}_X(\mathcal{D}_{0:t};\ \theta_X)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-af49-f60ab4587062" class="">For any variable , define an estimator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-b6f5-dd892ff920fb" class="">0) Standard interface (every operator must implement this)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8486-c6024c9a48c2" class="">Operator set : turning raw streams into computable loop variables (with error + falsification)</h2></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
